from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Optional

@dataclass
class CustomerMaster:
    CUST_ID: str
    CUST_CREDIT_SCORE: int
    CUST_TOTAL_LOANS: Decimal
    CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE: Decimal
    CUST_RISK_RATING: str

@dataclass
class AccountMaster:
    ACCT_ID: str
    ACCT_BALANCE: Decimal
    ACCT_OVERDRAFT_LIMIT: Decimal

@dataclass
class LoanMaster:
    LOAN_ID: str
    LOAN_DELINQUENT: bool
    LOAN_PAYMENT_AMOUNT: Decimal
    LOAN_CURRENT_BALANCE: Decimal
    LOAN_COLLATERAL_VALUE: Decimal
    LOAN_LTV_RATIO: Decimal

@dataclass
class InsuranceMaster:
    INS_POLICY_ID: str
    INS_LIFE: bool
    INS_HEALTH: bool
    INS_AUTO: bool
    INS_HOME: bool
    INS_UMBRELLA: bool
    INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT: Decimal
    INS_CLAIMS_COUNT: int
    INS_PREMIUM_AMOUNT: Decimal

@dataclass
class InvestmentMaster:
    INV_ID: str
    INV_QUANTITY: int
    INV_CURRENT_PRICE: Decimal
    INV_PURCHASE_PRICE: Decimal
    INV_MARKET_VALUE: Decimal
    INV_GAIN_LOSS: Decimal
    INV_DIVIDEND_RATE: Decimal
    INV_STOCKS: bool
    INV_BONDS: bool
    INV_MUTUAL_FUND: bool

@dataclass
class TransactionLog:
    TRAN_TIMESTAMP: str
    TRAN_TYPE: str
    TRAN_AMOUNT: Decimal
    TRAN_STATUS: str

@dataclass
class AuditTrail:
    AUD_TIMESTAMP: str

    def process_insurance(self):

           PERFORM 4100-PROCESS-POLICIES
           PERFORM 4200-CALCULATE-PREMIUMS
           PERFORM 4300-PROCESS-CLAIMS

           PERFORM 4500-RENEW-POLICIES."""

        self.calculate_premiums()
        self.process_claims()

        self.renew_policies()

    def process_policies(self):
        """4100-PROCESS-POLICIES.
           DISPLAY "PROCESSING INSURANCE POLICIES..."
           CONTINUE."""
        print("PROCESSING INSURANCE POLICIES...")

    def calculate_premiums(self):
        """4200-CALCULATE-PREMIUMS.
           DISPLAY "CALCULATING PREMIUMS..."
           SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
               READ INSURANCE-MASTER NEXT
                   AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
                   NOT AT END
                       PERFORM 4210-DETERMINE-BASE-PREMIUM
                       PERFORM 4220-APPLY-RISK-FACTOR
                       PERFORM 4230-CALCULATE-FINAL-PREMIUM
               END-READ
           END-PERFORM."""
        print("CALCULATING PREMIUMS...")
        self.WS_NOT_EOF = True
        self.WS_EOF = False

        while self.WS_NOT_EOF:
            try:
                insurance_record = self.INSURANCE_MASTER.pop(0)
                self.determine_base_premium(insurance_record)
                self.apply_risk_factor()
                self.calculate_final_premium(insurance_record)
            except IndexError:
                self.WS_EOF = True
                self.WS_NOT_EOF = False

    def determine_base_premium(self, insurance_record: InsuranceMaster):
        """4210-DETERMINE-BASE-PREMIUM.
           EVALUATE TRUE
               WHEN INS-LIFE
                   COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = 
                       INS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT / 1000 * WS-LIFE-RATE-PER-1000
               WHEN INS-HEALTH
                   MOVE WS-HEALTH-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-CALC-AMOUNT
               WHEN INS-AUTO
                   MOVE WS-AUTO-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-CALC-AMOUNT
               WHEN INS-HOME
                   COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = 
                       INS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT / 1000 * WS-HOME-RATE-PER-1000
               WHEN INS-UMBRELLA
                   MOVE WS-UMBRELLA-RATE TO WS-CALC-AMOUNT
           END-EVALUATE."""
        match True:
            case insurance_record.INS_LIFE:
                self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = insurance_record.INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT / Decimal("1000.00") * self.WS_LIFE_RATE_PER_1000
            case insurance_record.INS_HEALTH:
                self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = self.WS_HEALTH_BASE_PREMIUM
            case insurance_record.INS_AUTO:
                self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = self.WS_AUTO_BASE_PREMIUM
            case insurance_record.INS_HOME:
                self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = insurance_record.INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT / Decimal("1000.00") * self.WS_HOME_RATE_PER_1000
            case insurance_record.INS_UMBRELLA:
                self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = self.WS_UMBRELLA_RATE

    def apply_risk_factor(self):
        """4220-APPLY-RISK-FACTOR.
           IF INS-CLAIMS-COUNT > 2
               COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = WS-CALC-AMOUNT * 1.25
           END-IF."""
        insurance_record = self.INSURANCE_MASTER[-1]
        if insurance_record.INS_CLAIMS_COUNT > 2:
            self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT * Decimal("1.25")

    def calculate_final_premium(self, insurance_record: InsuranceMaster):
        """4230-CALCULATE-FINAL-PREMIUM.
           MOVE WS-CALC-AMOUNT TO INS-PREMIUM-AMOUNT
           ADD WS-CALC-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-PREMIUMS."""
        insurance_record.INS_PREMIUM_AMOUNT = self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT
        self.WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS += self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT

    def calculate_portfolio_value(self):
        """5200-CALCULATE-PORTFOLIO-VALUE.
           DISPLAY "CALCULATING PORTFOLIO VALUES..."
           SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
               READ INVESTMENT-MASTER NEXT
                   AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
                   NOT AT END
                       PERFORM 5210-CALCULATE-POSITION-VALUE
                       PERFORM 5220-CALCULATE-GAIN-LOSS
                       PERFORM 5230-UPDATE-TOTALS
               END-READ
           END-PERFORM."""
        print("CALCULATING PORTFOLIO VALUES...")
        self.WS_NOT_EOF = True
        self.WS_EOF = False

        while self.WS_NOT_EOF:
            try:
                investment_record = self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.pop(0)
                self.calculate_position_value(investment_record)
                self.calculate_gain_loss(investment_record)
                self.update_totals(investment_record)
            except IndexError:
                self.WS_EOF = True
                self.WS_NOT_EOF = False

    def calculate_position_value(self, investment_record: InvestmentMaster):
        """5210-CALCULATE-POSITION-VALUE.
           COMPUTE INV-MARKET-VALUE = 
               INV-QUANTITY * INV-CURRENT-PRICE."""
        investment_record.INV_MARKET_VALUE = Decimal(investment_record.INV_QUANTITY) * investment_record.INV_CURRENT_PRICE

    def calculate_gain_loss(self, investment_record: InvestmentMaster):
        """5220-CALCULATE-GAIN-LOSS.
           COMPUTE INV-GAIN-LOSS = 
               INV-MARKET-VALUE - (INV-QUANTITY * INV-PURCHASE-PRICE)."""
        investment_record.INV_GAIN_LOSS = investment_record.INV_MARKET_VALUE - (Decimal(investment_record.INV_QUANTITY) * investment_record.INV_PURCHASE_PRICE)

    def update_totals(self, investment_record: InvestmentMaster):
        """5230-UPDATE-TOTALS.
           ADD INV-MARKET-VALUE TO WS-TOTAL-INVESTMENTS."""
        self.WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += investment_record.INV_MARKET_VALUE

    def calculate_dividends(self):
        """5400-CALCULATE-DIVIDENDS.
           DISPLAY "CALCULATING DIVIDENDS..."
           SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
           PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
               READ INVESTMENT-MASTER NEXT
                   AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
                   NOT AT END
                       IF INV-DIVIDEND-RATE > 0
                           PERFORM 5410-COMPUTE-DIVIDEND
                           PERFORM 5420-POST-DIVIDEND
                       END-IF
               END-READ
           END-PERFORM."""
        print("CALCULATING DIVIDENDS...")
        self.WS_NOT_EOF = True
        self.WS_EOF = False

        while self.WS_NOT_EOF:
            try:
                investment_record = self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.pop(0)
                if investment_record.INV_DIVIDEND_RATE > 0:
                    self.compute_dividend(investment_record)
                    self.post_dividend()
            except IndexError:
                self.WS_EOF = True
                self.WS_NOT_EOF = False

    def compute_dividend(self, investment_record: InvestmentMaster):
        """5410-COMPUTE-DIVIDEND.
           COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = 
               INV-MARKET-VALUE * INV-DIVIDEND-RATE / 4."""
        self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT = investment_record.INV_MARKET_VALUE * investment_record.INV_DIVIDEND_RATE / Decimal("4.00")

    def post_dividend(self):
        """5420-POST-DIVIDEND.
           ADD WS-CALC-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-DIVIDENDS."""
        self.WS_TOTAL_DIVIDENDS += self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT

        self.REPORT_FILE.append(self.REPORT_LINE)

    def write_transaction(self):

        self.AUDIT_TRAIL.append(audit_record)

    def format_date(self):
        """8300-FORMAT-DATE.
           STRING WS-TEMP-DATE(1:4) DELIMITED SIZE
                  '-' DELIMITED SIZE
                  WS-TEMP-DATE(5:2) DELIMITED SIZE
                  '-' DELIMITED SIZE
                  WS-TEMP-DATE(7:2) DELIMITED SIZE
                  INTO WS-FORMATTED-DATE."""
        self.WS_FORMATTED_DATE = f"{self.WS_TEMP_DATE[0:4]}-{self.WS_TEMP_DATE[4:6]}-{self.WS_TEMP_DATE[6:8]}"

    def validate_account(self):

            self.WS_INVALID = True

    def calculate_tax(self):
        """8500-CALCULATE-TAX.
           EVALUATE TRUE
               WHEN WS-CALC-AMOUNT <= WS-BRACKET-1-MAX
                   COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
                       WS-CALC-AMOUNT * WS-BRACKET-1-RATE
               WHEN WS-CALC-AMOUNT <= WS-BRACKET-2-MAX
                   COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
                       (WS-BRACKET-1-MAX * WS-BRACKET-1-RATE) +
                       ((WS-CALC-AMOUNT - WS-BRACKET-1-MAX) * 
                        WS-BRACKET-2-RATE)
               WHEN WS-CALC-AMOUNT <= WS-BRACKET-3-MAX
                   COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
                       (WS-BRACKET-1-MAX * WS-BRACKET-1-RATE) +
                       ((WS-BRACKET-2-MAX - WS-BRACKET-1-MAX) * 
                        WS-BRACKET-2-RATE) +
                       ((WS-CALC-AMOUNT - WS-BRACKET-2-MAX) * 
                        WS-BRACKET-3-RATE)
               WHEN OTHER
                   COMPUTE WS-CALC-TAX = 
                       WS-CALC-AMOUNT * WS-BRACKET-5-RATE
           END-EVALUATE."""
        match True:
            case self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= self.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX:
                self.WS_CALC_TAX = self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT * self.WS_BRACKET_1_RATE
            case self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= self.WS_BRACKET_2_MAX:
                self.WS_CALC_TAX = (self.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * self.WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + ((self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT - self.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * self.WS_BRACKET_2_RATE)
            case self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= self.WS_BRACKET_3_MAX:
                self.WS_CALC_TAX = (self.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * self.WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + ((self.WS_BRACKET_2_MAX - self.WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * self.WS_BRACKET_2_RATE) + ((self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT - self.WS_BRACKET_2_MAX) * self.WS_BRACKET_3_RATE)
            case _:
                self.WS_CALC_TAX = self.WS_CALC_AMOUNT * self.WS_BRACKET_5_RATE

    def termination(self):
        """9000-TERMINATION.
           PERFORM 9100-CLOSE-FILES
           PERFORM 9200-DISPLAY-STATISTICS
           DISPLAY "MEGA-ENTERPRISE SYSTEM TERMINATED NORMALLY"."""
        self.close_files()
        self.display_statistics()
        print("MEGA-ENTERPRISE SYSTEM TERMINATED NORMALLY")

    def close_files(self):
        """9100-CLOSE-FILES.
           CLOSE CUSTOMER-MASTER
           CLOSE ACCOUNT-MASTER
           CLOSE LOAN-MASTER
           CLOSE INSURANCE-MASTER
           CLOSE INVESTMENT-MASTER
           CLOSE TRANSACTION-LOG
           CLOSE AUDIT-TRAIL
           CLOSE REPORT-FILE."""
        self.CUSTOMER_MASTER.clear()
        self.ACCOUNT_MASTER.clear()
        self.LOAN_MASTER.clear()
        self.INSURANCE_MASTER.clear()
        self.INVESTMENT_MASTER.clear()
        self.TRANSACTION_LOG.clear()
        self.AUDIT_TRAIL.clear()
        self.REPORT_FILE.clear()

    def display_statistics(self):
        """9200-DISPLAY-STATISTICS.
           DISPLAY "============================================"
           DISPLAY "       PROCESSING STATISTICS                "
           DISPLAY "============================================"
           MOVE WS-CUST-COUNT TO WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
           DISPLAY "CUSTOMERS PROCESSED:    " WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
           MOVE WS-ACCT-COUNT TO WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
           DISPLAY "ACCOUNTS PROCESSED:     " WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
           MOVE WS-TRAN-COUNT TO WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
           DISPLAY "TRANSACTIONS PROCESSED: " WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
           MOVE WS-LOAN-COUNT TO WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
           DISPLAY "LOANS PROCESSED:        " WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
           MOVE WS-ERROR-COUNT TO WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
           DISPLAY "ERRORS ENCOUNTERED:     " WS-FORMATTED-COUNT
           DISPLAY "============================================"
           MOVE WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS TO WS-FORMATTED-AMOUNT
           DISPLAY "TOTAL DEPOSITS:    " WS-FORMATTED-AMOUNT
           MOVE WS-TOTAL-WITHDRAWALS TO WS-FORMATTED-AMOUNT
           DISPLAY "TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: " WS-FORMATTED-AMOUNT
           MOVE WS-TOTAL-INTEREST TO WS-FORMATTED-AMOUNT
           DISPLAY "TOTAL INTEREST:    " WS-FORMATTED-AMOUNT
           MOVE WS-TOTAL-FEES TO WS-FORMATTED-AMOUNT
           DISPLAY "TOTAL FEES:        " WS-FORMATTED-AMOUNT
           DISPLAY "============================================"."""
        print("============================================")
        print("       PROCESSING STATISTICS                ")
        print("============================================")
        self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT = str(self.WS_CUST_COUNT)
        print(f"CUSTOMERS PROCESSED:    {self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT}")
        self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT = str(self.WS_ACCT_COUNT)
        print(f"ACCOUNTS PROCESSED:     {self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT}")
        self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT = str(self.WS_TRAN_COUNT)
        print(f"TRANSACTIONS PROCESSED: {self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT}")
        self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT = str(self.WS_LOAN_COUNT)
        print(f"LOANS PROCESSED:        {self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT}")
        self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT = str(self.WS_ERROR_COUNT)
        print(f"ERRORS ENCOUNTERED:     {self.WS_FORMATTED_COUNT}")
        print("

def customer_segmentation():
    """9310-CUSTOMER-SEGMENTATION.
    DISPLAY "SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS..."
    SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
    PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
        READ CUSTOMER-MASTER NEXT
            AT END SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
            NOT AT END
                PERFORM 9311-CALCULATE-CLV
                PERFORM 9312-ASSIGN-SEGMENT
        END-READ
    END-PERFORM.
    """
    global global_vars, customer_master
    print("SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS...")
    global_vars.ws_not_eof = True
    while not global_vars.ws_eof:
        # Simulate reading from customer master
        # In a real implementation, this would involve file I/O or database access
        # For this example, we'll just create a dummy customer and set EOF
        # after one iteration
        if global_vars.ws_not_eof: # Simulate NOT AT END
            calculate_clv()
            assign_segment()
            global_vars.ws_eof = True # Simulate AT END
            global_vars.ws_not_eof = False
        else:
            global_vars.ws_eof = True

def calculate_clv():
    """9311-CALCULATE-CLV.
    COMPUTE WS-CALC-RESULT = 
        (CUST-TOTAL-BALANCE * WS-SAVINGS-RATE) +
        (CUST-TOTAL-LOANS * WS-PERSONAL-RATE) +
        (CUST-TOTAL-INVESTMENTS * 0.01).
    """
    global global_vars, customer_master
    global_vars.ws_calc_result = (
        (customer_master.cust_total_balance * global_vars.ws_savings_rate) +
        (customer_master.cust_total_loans * global_vars.ws_personal_rate) +
        (customer_master.cust_total_investments * Decimal("0.01"))
    )

def assign_segment():
    """9312-ASSIGN-SEGMENT.
    EVALUATE TRUE
        WHEN WS-CALC-RESULT > 10000
            MOVE 'PLATINUM' TO WS-TEMP-CODE
        WHEN WS-CALC-RESULT > 5000
            MOVE 'GOLD' TO WS-TEMP-CODE
        WHEN WS-CALC-RESULT > 1000
            MOVE 'SILVER' TO WS-TEMP-CODE
        WHEN OTHER
            MOVE 'BRONZE' TO WS-TEMP-CODE
    END-EVALUATE.
    """
    global global_vars
    ws_calc_result = global_vars.ws_calc_result
    if ws_calc_result > 10000:
        global_vars.ws_temp_code = 'PLATINUM'
    elif ws_calc_result > 5000:
        global_vars.ws_temp_code = 'GOLD'
    elif ws_calc_result > 1000:
        global_vars.ws_temp_code = 'SILVER'
    else:
        global_vars.ws_temp_code = 'BRONZE'

def default_prediction():
    """9343-DEFAULT-PREDICTION.
    IF LOAN-DELINQUENT
        ADD 25 TO WS-CALC-RESULT
    END-IF
    IF CUST-CREDIT-SCORE < 600
        ADD 30 TO WS-CALC-RESULT
    END-IF.
    """
    global global_vars, customer_master
    if global_vars.loan_delinquent:
        global_vars.ws_calc_result += Decimal("25.00")
    if customer_master.cust_credit_score < 600:
        global_vars.ws_calc_result += Decimal("30.00")

def sweep_accounts():
    """9632-SWEEP-ACCOUNTS.
    IF ACCT-BALANCE > ACCT-MIN-BALANCE
        COMPUTE WS-CALC-AMOUNT = ACCT-BALANCE - ACCT-MIN-BALANCE
        SUBTRACT WS-CALC-AMOUNT FROM ACCT-BALANCE
        ADD WS-CALC-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-INVESTMENTS
    END-IF.
    """
    global global_vars
    if global_vars.acct_balance > global_vars.acct_min_balance:
        global_vars.ws_calc_amount = global_vars.acct_balance - global_vars.acct_min_balance
        global_vars.acct_balance -= global_vars.ws_calc_amount
        global_vars.ws_total_investments += global_vars.ws_calc_amount

def c100_transaction_monitoring():
    """C100-TRANSACTION-MONITORING."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    print("MONITORING TRANSACTIONS...")  # DISPLAY "MONITORING TRANSACTIONS..."
    WS_NOT_EOF = True  # SET WS-NOT-EOF TO TRUE
    WS_EOF = False
    while WS_NOT_EOF:  # PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF
        transaction = read_transaction_log()
        if transaction is None:
            WS_EOF = True  # SET WS-EOF TO TRUE
            WS_NOT_EOF = False
        else:
            global TRAN_AMOUNT
            TRAN_AMOUNT = transaction.transaction_amount
            c110_rule_based_detection()  # PERFORM C110-RULE-BASED-DETECTION
            c120_behavior_analysis()  # PERFORM C120-BEHAVIOR-ANALYSIS
            c130_network_analysis()  # PERFORM C130-NETWORK-ANALYSIS


def c110_rule_based_detection():
    """C110-RULE-BASED-DETECTION."""
    if TRAN_AMOUNT >= Decimal(10000):  # IF TRAN-AMOUNT >= 10000
        c111_flag_ctr()  # PERFORM C111-FLAG-CTR
    if Decimal(5000) <= TRAN_AMOUNT < Decimal(10000):  # IF TRAN-AMOUNT >= 5000 AND TRAN-AMOUNT < 10000
        c112_check_structuring()  # PERFORM C112-CHECK-STRUCTURING


def c111_flag_ctr():
    """C111-FLAG-CTR."""
    global WS_PROCESS_COUNT
    WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-PROCESS-COUNT.


def c112_check_structuring():
    """C112-CHECK-STRUCTURING."""
    global WS_ERROR_COUNT
    WS_ERROR_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO WS-ERROR-COUNT.

def d110_classification():
    """D110-CLASSIFICATION."""
    global CUST_RISK_RATING
    if CUST_CREDIT_SCORE > 750:  # IF CUST-CREDIT-SCORE > 750
        CUST_RISK_RATING = 'A'  # MOVE 'A' TO CUST-RISK-RATING
    elif CUST_CREDIT_SCORE > 650:  # ELSE IF CUST-CREDIT-SCORE > 650
        CUST_RISK_RATING = 'B'  # MOVE 'B' TO CUST-RISK-RATING
    elif CUST_CREDIT_SCORE > 550:  # ELSE IF CUST-CREDIT-SCORE > 550
        CUST_RISK_RATING = 'C'  # MOVE 'C' TO CUST-RISK-RATING
    else:  # ELSE
        CUST_RISK_RATING = 'D'  # MOVE 'D' TO CUST-RISK-RATING


def d120_regression():
    """D120-REGRESSION."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT
    WS_CALC_RESULT = (CUST_CREDIT_SCORE * 10) + (CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE / Decimal(1000)) - (
            CUST_TOTAL_LOANS / Decimal(2000))  # COMPUTE WS-CALC-RESULT = (CUST-CREDIT-SCORE * 10) + (CUST-TOTAL-BALANCE / 1000) - (CUST-TOTAL-LOANS / 2000).

def process_0000_main_control() -> None:
    """0000-MAIN-CONTROL.
    PERFORM 1000-INITIALIZATION
    PERFORM 2000-PROCESS-TRANSACTIONS
       UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
    PERFORM 9000-FINALIZATION
    STOP RUN."""
    process_1000_initialization()
    while ws.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        process_2000_process_transactions()
    process_9000_finalization()
    #STOP RUN translates to program termination, so no explicit action

def process_1000_initialization() -> None:
    """1000-INITIALIZATION.
    INITIALIZE WS-WORK-AREAS
    INITIALIZE WS-COUNTERS
    INITIALIZE WS-TOTALS
    MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-CURRENT-DATETIME
    MOVE WS-CURR-YEAR  TO RPT-YEAR
    MOVE WS-CURR-MONTH TO RPT-MONTH
    MOVE WS-CURR-DAY   TO RPT-DAY
    PERFORM 1100-OPEN-FILES
    PERFORM 1200-READ-PARAMETERS
    PERFORM 1300-INITIALIZE-TABLES
    PERFORM 1400-LOAD-REFERENCE-DATA."""
    ws.WS_WORK_AREAS = "" # Placeholder for initializing work areas
    ws.WS_COUNTERS = "" # Placeholder for initializing counters
    ws.WS_TOTALS = "" # Placeholder for initializing totals
    ws.WS_CURRENT_DATETIME = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d") #Python doesn't have currdate, use datetime lib
    ws.WS_CURR_YEAR  = datetime.now().strftime("%Y")
    ws.WS_CURR_MONTH = datetime.now().strftime("%m")
    ws.WS_CURR_DAY   = datetime.now().strftime("%d")
    process_1100_open_files()
    process_1200_read_parameters()
    process_1300_initialize_tables()
    process_1400_load_reference_data()

def process_1100_open_files() -> None:
    """1100-OPEN-FILES.
    OPEN INPUT  CUSTOMER-FILE
    OPEN INPUT  ACCOUNT-FILE
    OPEN INPUT  TRANSACTION-FILE
    OPEN OUTPUT REPORT-FILE
    OPEN OUTPUT ERROR-FILE
    OPEN I-O    MASTER-FILE
    IF WS-FILE-STATUS NOT = '00'
       MOVE 'FILE OPEN ERROR' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
       PERFORM 9500-ABORT-PROCESS
    END-IF."""
    # In Python, file opening is typically handled within the relevant functions
    # For this example, we assume the files are "opened" implicitly when needed.
    # Check WS-FILE-STATUS after attempting to open the file in real implementation.
    # Assuming 00 is success
    ws.WS_FILE_STATUS = '00'  # Dummy Status, proper implementation requires file access and status check
    if ws.WS_FILE_STATUS != '00':
        ws.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'FILE OPEN ERROR'
        process_9500_abort_process()

def process_1200_read_parameters() -> None:
    """1200-READ-PARAMETERS.
    ACCEPT WS-PARAM-DATE FROM DATE
    ACCEPT WS-PARAM-TIME FROM TIME
    MOVE 'BATCH-001' TO WS-JOB-ID
    MOVE 'PRODUCTION' TO WS-ENV-TYPE
    COMPUTE WS-PROCESS-DATE = 
       FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-PARAM-DATE)."""
    ws.WS_PARAM_DATE = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d") # Simulate ACCEPT FROM DATE
    ws.WS_PARAM_TIME = datetime.now().strftime("%H%M%S") # Simulate ACCEPT FROM TIME
    ws.WS_JOB_ID = 'BATCH-001'
    ws.WS_ENV_TYPE = 'PRODUCTION'
    ws.WS_PROCESS_DATE = int(datetime.strptime(ws.WS_PARAM_DATE, "%Y%m%d").toordinal()) #Simulate INTEGER-OF-DATE

def process_1300_initialize_tables() -> None:
    """1300-INITIALIZE-TABLES.
    PERFORM VARYING WS-TBL-IDX FROM 1 BY 1
       UNTIL WS-TBL-IDX > 100
       INITIALIZE RATE-TABLE-ENTRY(WS-TBL-IDX)
       MOVE ZEROES TO RT-RATE(WS-TBL-IDX)
       MOVE SPACES TO RT-CODE(WS-TBL-IDX)
    END-PERFORM
    PERFORM VARYING WS-TBL-IDX FROM 1 BY 1
       UNTIL WS-TBL-IDX > 50
       INITIALIZE BRANCH-TABLE-ENTRY(WS-TBL-IDX)
    END-PERFORM."""
    for ws.WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 101):
        ws.RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[ws.WS_TBL_IDX-1] = RateTableEntry()  #Python list 0-index
        ws.RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[ws.WS_TBL_IDX-1].RT_RATE = Decimal("0.00")
        ws.RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[ws.WS_TBL_IDX-1].RT_CODE = ""

    for ws.WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 51):
        ws.BRANCH_TABLE_ENTRY[ws.WS_TBL_IDX-1] = BranchTableEntry() #Python list 0-index

def process_1400_load_reference_data() -> None:
    """1400-LOAD-REFERENCE-DATA.
    MOVE 1 TO WS-TBL-IDX
    PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y' 
                   OR WS-TBL-IDX > 100
       READ REFERENCE-FILE INTO WS-REF-RECORD
          AT END
             MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
          NOT AT END
             MOVE WS-REF-CODE TO RT-CODE(WS-TBL-IDX)
             MOVE WS-REF-RATE TO RT-RATE(WS-TBL-IDX)
             ADD 1 TO WS-TBL-IDX
       END-READ
    END-PERFORM
    MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG."""
    ws.WS_TBL_IDX = 1
    while ws.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y' and ws.WS_TBL_IDX <= 100:
        ws.WS_REF_RECORD = read_reference_file() #Simulate read file operation
        if ws.WS_REF_RECORD is None:
            ws.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            ws.RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[ws.WS_TBL_IDX-1].RT_CODE = ws.WS_REF_RECORD.WS_REF_CODE
            ws.RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[ws.WS_TBL_IDX-1].RT_RATE = ws.WS_REF_RECORD.WS_REF_RATE
            ws.WS_TBL_IDX += 1
    ws.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def process_2000_process_transactions() -> None:
    """2000-PROCESS-TRANSACTIONS.
    READ TRANSACTION-FILE INTO WS-TRANSACTION-REC
       AT END
          MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
       NOT AT END
          ADD 1 TO WS-TRANS-COUNT
          PERFORM 2100-VALIDATE-TRANSACTION
          IF WS-VALID-FLAG = 'Y'
             PERFORM 2200-PROCESS-BY-TYPE
          ELSE
             PERFORM 2900-HANDLE-ERROR
          END-IF
    END-READ."""
    ws.WS_TRANSACTION_REC = read_transaction_file() #Simulate reading from file
    if ws.WS_TRANSACTION_REC is None:
        ws.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    else:
        ws.WS_TRANS_COUNT += 1
        process_2100_validate_transaction()
        if ws.WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':
            process_2200_process_by_type()
        else:
            process_2900_handle_error()

def process_2100_validate_transaction() -> None:
    """2100-VALIDATE-TRANSACTION.
    MOVE 'Y' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
    IF TXN-ACCOUNT-ID = SPACES OR LOW-VALUES
       MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
       MOVE 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
       EXIT PARAGRAPH
    END-IF
    IF TXN-AMOUNT IS NOT NUMERIC
       MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
       MOVE 'INVALID AMOUNT' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
       EXIT PARAGRAPH
    END-IF
    IF TXN-TYPE NOT = 'D' AND TXN-TYPE NOT = 'W'
       AND TXN-TYPE NOT = 'T' AND TXN-TYPE NOT = 'I'
       MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
       MOVE 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
    END-IF
    PERFORM 2150-VALIDATE-ACCOUNT-EXISTS
    PERFORM 2160-VALIDATE-BUSINESS-RULES."""
    ws.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'Y'
    if ws.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID == "" or ws.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID < chr(0): # chr(0) = low-values
        ws.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        ws.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID'
        return  #Simulate EXIT PARAGRAPH
    try:
        #Check for numeric
        txn_amount = Decimal(str(ws.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT))
    except:
        ws.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        ws.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
        return  #Simulate EXIT PARAGRAPH

    if ws.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TYPE not in ('D', 'W', 'T', 'I'):
        ws.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        ws.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE'

    process_2150_validate_account_exists()
    process_2160_validate_business_rules()

def process_2150_validate_account_exists() -> None:
    """2150-VALIDATE-ACCOUNT-EXISTS.
    MOVE TXN-ACCOUNT-ID TO WS-SEARCH-KEY
    PERFORM 5000-SEARCH-ACCOUNT
    IF WS-FOUND-FLAG = 'N'
       MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
       MOVE 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
    END-IF."""
    ws.WS_SEARCH_KEY = ws.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID
    process_5000_search_account()
    if ws.WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'N':
        ws.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        ws.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'

def process_2160_validate_business_rules() -> None:
    """2160-VALIDATE-BUSINESS-RULES.
    IF TXN-TYPE = 'W'
       IF TXN-AMOUNT > WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
          MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
          MOVE 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
       END-IF
    END-IF
    IF TXN-AMOUNT > 1000000
       MOVE 'N' TO WS-VALID-FLAG
       MOVE 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT' TO WS-ERROR-MSG
    END-IF."""

    if ws.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TYPE == 'W':
        try:
            txn_amount = Decimal(str(ws.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT))
        except:
            txn_amount = Decimal("0.00")

@dataclass
class Holding:
    """
    10 HOLD-SYMBOL          PIC X(10).
    10 HOLD-NAME            PIC X(50).

WS_VEHICLE_AGE: int = 0

WS_TARGET_BONDS_PCT: Decimal = Decimal("0")
WS_PROPERTY_TAX: Decimal = Decimal("0")

def calculate_pmi():
    """10335-CALCULATE-PMI."""
    global WS_PMI_AMOUNT
    match True:
        case WS_LTV_RATIO > 95:
            WS_PMI_AMOUNT = WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.0125") / 12
        case WS_LTV_RATIO > 90:
            WS_PMI_AMOUNT = WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.0100") / 12
        case WS_LTV_RATIO > 85:
            WS_PMI_AMOUNT = WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.0075") / 12
        case _:
            WS_PMI_AMOUNT = WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.0050") / 12

def evaluate_history():
    """10340-EVALUATE-HISTORY."""
    global WS_RISK_SCORE, WS_FACTOR_1, WS_FACTOR_2, WS_FACTOR_3
    if WS_LATE_90_DAYS > 0:
        WS_RISK_SCORE -= 50
        WS_FACTOR_1 = 'SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY'
    if WS_LATE_60_DAYS > 2:
        WS_RISK_SCORE -= 30
        WS_FACTOR_2 = '60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES'
    if WS_LATE_30_DAYS > 5:
        WS_RISK_SCORE -= 20
        WS_FACTOR_3 = 'MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES'

def calculate_final_risk():
    """10350-CALCULATE-FINAL-RISK."""
    global WS_RISK_SCORE, WS_RISK_CATEGORY
    WS_RISK_SCORE = WS_RISK_SCORE / 4
    match True:
        case WS_RISK_SCORE >= 80:
            WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'LOW RISK'
        case WS_RISK_SCORE >= 60:
            WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'MODERATE'
        case WS_RISK_SCORE >= 40:
            WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'ELEVATED'
        case _:
            WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'HIGH RISK'

def determine_approval():
    """10400-DETERMINE-APPROVAL."""
    global WS_CREDIT_TIER, WS_APPROVAL_STATUS, WS_CONDITIONS, WS_RISK_CATEGORY
    if WS_CREDIT_TIER == 'F':
        WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'D'
        WS_CONDITIONS = 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW'
        return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH
    if WS_RISK_CATEGORY == 'HIGH RISK':
        WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'D'
        WS_CONDITIONS = 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED'
        return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH
    if WS_DTI_RATIO > 50:
        WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'D'
        WS_CONDITIONS = 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH'
        return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH
    WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'A'
    calculate_approved_terms()

def calculate_approved_terms():
    """10450-CALCULATE-APPROVED-TERMS."""
    global WS_LOAN_AMOUNT, WS_APPROVED_AMOUNT, WS_CREDIT_TIER, WS_BASE_RATE, WS_APPROVED_RATE, WS_RISK_CATEGORY
    WS_APPROVED_AMOUNT = WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
    match WS_CREDIT_TIER:
        case 'A':
            WS_APPROVED_RATE = WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal("0.00")
        case 'B':
            WS_APPROVED_RATE = WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal("0.50")
        case 'C':
            WS_APPROVED_RATE = WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal("1.50")
        case 'D':
            WS_APPROVED_RATE = WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal("3.00")
    if WS_RISK_CATEGORY == 'ELEVATED':
        WS_APPROVED_RATE += Decimal("0.50")

def generate_loan_terms():
    """10500-GENERATE-LOAN-TERMS."""
    global WS_APPROVED_RATE, WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE, WS_MONTHLY_RATE, WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR, WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT, WS_LOAN_AMOUNT, WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS, WS_LOAN_PRINCIPAL_BAL
    WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE = WS_APPROVED_RATE
    WS_MONTHLY_RATE = WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE / 1200
    WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR = (1 + WS_MONTHLY_RATE) ** WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS
    WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT = WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * WS_MONTHLY_RATE * WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR / (WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR - 1)
    WS_LOAN_PRINCIPAL_BAL = WS_LOAN_AMOUNT

def create_amortization():
    """10600-CREATE-AMORTIZATION."""
    global WS_LOAN_AMOUNT, WS_RUNNING_BALANCE, WS_PAYMENT_DATE, WS_AMORT_IDX, WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS, AMORT_INTEREST, AMORT_PRINCIPAL, AMORT_BALANCE, AMORT_PAYMENT_NUM, AMORT_PAYMENT_AMT, AMORT_ESCROW, AMORT_TOTAL_PMT, AMORT_PAYMENT_DATE
    WS_RUNNING_BALANCE = WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
    WS_PAYMENT_DATE = int(datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d"))

    amortization_schedule = AmortizationSchedule(
        amort_interest=[Decimal("0")] * (WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS + 1),
        amort_principal=[Decimal("0")] * (WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS + 1),
        amort_balance=[Decimal("0")] * (WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS + 1),
        amort_payment_num=[0] * (WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS + 1),
        amort_payment_amt=[Decimal("0")] * (WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS + 1),
        amort_escrow=[Decimal("0")] * (WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS + 1),
        amort_total_pmt=[Decimal("0")] * (WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS + 1),
        amort_payment_date=[0] * (WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS + 1)
    )
    
    for WS_AMORT_IDX in range(1, WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS + 1):
        calculate_payment_split(amortization_schedule, WS_AMORT_IDX)

def calculate_payment_split(amortization_schedule: AmortizationSchedule, ws_amort_idx: int):
    """10650-CALCULATE-PAYMENT-SPLIT."""
    global WS_RUNNING_BALANCE, WS_MONTHLY_RATE, WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT, LOAN_MORTGAGE, WS_PROPERTY_TAX, WS_INSURANCE_PREMIUM, WS_PMI_AMOUNT

    amortization_schedule.amort_interest[ws_amort_idx] = WS_RUNNING_BALANCE * WS_MONTHLY_RATE
    amortization_schedule.amort_principal[ws_amort_idx] = WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT - amortization_schedule.amort_interest[ws_amort_idx]
    WS_RUNNING_BALANCE -= amortization_schedule.amort_principal[ws_amort_idx]
    amortization_schedule.amort_balance[ws_amort_idx] = WS_RUNNING_BALANCE
    amortization_schedule.amort_payment_num[ws_amort_idx] = ws_amort_idx
    amortization_schedule.amort_payment_amt[ws_amort_idx] = WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT

    if LOAN_MORTGAGE:
        amortization_schedule.amort_escrow[ws_amort_idx] = (WS_PROPERTY_TAX + WS_INSURANCE_PREMIUM) / 12
        amortization_schedule.amort_total_pmt[ws_amort_idx] = WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT + amortization_schedule.amort_escrow[ws_amort_idx] + WS_PMI_AMOUNT
    else:
        amortization_schedule.amort_total_pmt[ws_amort_idx] = WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT

    advance_payment_date(amortization_schedule, ws_amort_idx)

def advance_payment_date(amortization_schedule: AmortizationSchedule, ws_amort_idx: int):
    """10660-ADVANCE-PAYMENT-DATE."""
    global WS_PAYMENT_MONTH, WS_PAYMENT_YEAR
    WS_PAYMENT_MONTH += 1
    if WS_PAYMENT_MONTH > 12:
        WS_PAYMENT_MONTH = 1
        WS_PAYMENT_YEAR += 1
    amortization_schedule.amort_payment_date[ws_amort_idx] = WS_PAYMENT_YEAR * 10000 + WS_PAYMENT_MONTH * 100 + 1

def finalize_loan():
    """10700-FINALIZE-LOAN."""
    global WS_LOAN_START_DATE, WS_LOAN_END_DATE, WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS, WS_LOAN_STATUS
    WS_LOAN_START_DATE = int(datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d"))
    WS_LOAN_END_DATE = WS_LOAN_START_DATE + (WS_LOAN_TERM_MONTHS * 30)
    WS_LOAN_STATUS = 'A'
    create_loan_record()
    disburse_funds()
    send_confirmation()

def load_portfolio():
    """11100-LOAD-PORTFOLIO."""
    global WS_HOLD_IDX, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_HOLDING_REC, WS_HOLDING, WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT
    WS_HOLD_IDX = 1
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_HOLD_IDX <= 100 and WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            # Simulate reading from HOLDINGS-FILE
            WS_HOLDING_REC = HoldingRec(hold_symbol=f"SYM{WS_HOLD_IDX}", hold_shares=Decimal(WS_HOLD_IDX), hold_cost_per_share=Decimal(WS_HOLD_IDX + 1), hold_current_price=Decimal(WS_HOLD_IDX + 2), hold_market_value=Decimal(WS_HOLD_IDX + 3), hold_gain_loss=Decimal(WS_HOLD_IDX + 4), hold_pct_change=Decimal(WS_HOLD_IDX + 5), hold_purchase_date=20240101, hold_type='STK')
            WS_HOLDING[WS_HOLD_IDX] = WS_HOLDING_REC
            WS_HOLD_IDX += 1
        except:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT = WS_HOLD_IDX - 1

def update_market_prices():
    """11200-UPDATE-MARKET-PRICES."""
    global WS_HOLD_IDX, WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT, WS_QUOTE_SYMBOL, HOLD_SYMBOL, HOLD_CURRENT_PRICE
    for WS_HOLD_IDX in range(1, WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT + 1):
        WS_QUOTE_SYMBOL = HOLD_SYMBOL[WS_HOLD_IDX]
        get_quote()
        HOLD_CURRENT_PRICE[WS_HOLD_IDX] = WS_QUOTE_PRICE

def get_quote():
    """11250-GET-QUOTE."""
    global WS_QUOTE_SYMBOL, QUOTE_REQUEST_SYMBOL, QUOTE_RESPONSE, WS_QUOTE_PRICE
    QUOTE_REQUEST_SYMBOL = WS_QUOTE_SYMBOL
    #CALL 'GETQUOTE' USING QUOTE-REQUEST QUOTE-RESPONSE  (Simulated)
    QUOTE_RESPONSE = QuoteResponse(quote_last_price = Decimal("123.45"), quote_response_status = 'OK')
    if QUOTE_RESPONSE.quote_response_status == 'OK':
        WS_QUOTE_PRICE = QUOTE_RESPONSE.quote_last_price
    else:
        WS_QUOTE_PRICE = Decimal("0")

def calculate_values():
    """11300-CALCULATE-VALUES."""
    global WS_TOTAL_VALUE, WS_COST_BASIS, WS_UNREALIZED_GAIN, WS_HOLD_IDX, WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT
    WS_TOTAL_VALUE = Decimal("0")
    WS_COST_BASIS = Decimal("0")
    WS_UNREALIZED_GAIN = Decimal("0")
    for WS_HOLD_IDX in range(1, WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT + 1):
        calculate_holding_value()

def calculate_holding_value():
    """11350-CALCULATE-HOLDING-VALUE."""
    global WS_HOLD_IDX, HOLD_MARKET_VALUE, HOLD_SHARES, HOLD_CURRENT_PRICE, WS_HOLD_COST, HOLD_COST_PER_SHARE, HOLD_GAIN_LOSS, HOLD_PCT_CHANGE, WS_TOTAL_VALUE, WS_COST_BASIS, WS_UNREALIZED_GAIN
    HOLD_MARKET_VALUE[WS_HOLD_IDX] = HOLD_SHARES[WS_HOLD_IDX] * HOLD_CURRENT_PRICE[WS_HOLD_IDX]
    WS_HOLD_COST = HOLD_SHARES[WS_HOLD_IDX] * HOLD_COST_PER_SHARE[WS_HOLD_IDX]
    HOLD_GAIN_LOSS[WS_HOLD_IDX] = HOLD_MARKET_VALUE[WS_HOLD_IDX] - WS_HOLD_COST
    if WS_HOLD_COST > 0:
        HOLD_PCT_CHANGE[WS_HOLD_IDX] = (HOLD_GAIN_LOSS[WS_HOLD_IDX] / WS_HOLD_COST) * 100
    else:
        HOLD_PCT_CHANGE[WS_HOLD_IDX] = Decimal("0")
    WS_TOTAL_VALUE += HOLD_MARKET_VALUE[WS_HOLD_IDX]
    WS_COST_BASIS += WS_HOLD_COST
    WS_UNREALIZED_GAIN += HOLD_GAIN_LOSS[WS_HOLD_IDX]

def rebalance_check():
    """11400-REBALANCE-CHECK."""
    global WS_REBALANCE_NEEDED
    calculate_current_allocation()
    compare_to_target()
    if WS_REBALANCE_NEEDED == 'Y':
        generate_rebalance_trades()

def calculate_current_allocation():
    """11410-CALCULATE-CURRENT-ALLOCATION."""
    global WS_STOCKS_VALUE, WS_BONDS_VALUE, WS_CASH_VALUE, WS_HOLD_IDX, WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT, HOLD_TYPE, HOLD_MARKET_VALUE, WS_TOTAL_VALUE, WS_STOCKS_PCT, WS_BONDS_PCT, WS_CASH_PCT
    WS_STOCKS_VALUE = Decimal("0")
    WS_BONDS_VALUE = Decimal("0")
    WS_CASH_VALUE = Decimal("0")
    for WS_HOLD_IDX in range(1, WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT + 1):
        match HOLD_TYPE[WS_HOLD_IDX]:
            case 'STK':
                WS_STOCKS_VALUE += HOLD_MARKET_VALUE[WS_HOLD_IDX]
            case 'BND':
                WS_BONDS_VALUE += HOLD_MARKET_VALUE[WS_HOLD_IDX]
            case 'CSH':
                WS_CASH_VALUE += HOLD_MARKET_VALUE[WS_HOLD_IDX]
    WS_STOCKS_PCT = (WS_STOCKS_VALUE / WS_TOTAL_VALUE) * 100
    WS_BONDS_PCT = (WS_BONDS_VALUE / WS_TOTAL_VALUE) * 100
    WS_CASH_PCT = (WS_CASH_VALUE / WS_TOTAL_VALUE) * 100

def compare_to_target():
    """11420-COMPARE-TO-TARGET."""
    global WS_REBALANCE_NEEDED, WS_STOCKS_PCT, WS_TARGET_STOCKS_PCT, WS_BONDS_PCT, WS_TARGET_BONDS_PCT, WS_STOCKS_DIFF, WS_BONDS_DIFF
    WS_REBALANCE_NEEDED = 'N'
    WS_STOCKS_DIFF = WS_STOCKS_PCT - WS_TARGET_STOCKS_PCT
    WS_BONDS_DIFF = WS_BONDS_PCT - WS_TARGET_BONDS_PCT
    if abs(WS_STOCKS_DIFF) > 5:
        WS_REBALANCE_NEEDED = 'Y'
    if abs(WS_BONDS_DIFF) > 5:
        WS_REBALANCE_NEEDED = 'Y'

def generate_rebalance_trades():
    """11430-GENERATE-REBALANCE-TRADES."""
    global WS_STOCKS_DIFF, WS_TOTAL_VALUE, WS_SELL_AMOUNT, WS_BUY_AMOUNT
    if WS_STOCKS_DIFF > 0:
        WS_SELL_AMOUNT = WS_TOTAL_VALUE * WS_STOCKS_DIFF / 100
        create_sell_order()
    else:
        WS_BUY_AMOUNT = WS_TOTAL_VALUE * (0 - WS_STOCKS_DIFF) / 100
        create_buy_order()

def create_sell_order():
    """11440-CREATE-SELL-ORDER."""
    global WS_TRADE_TYPE, WS_ORDER_TYPE, WS_SELL_AMOUNT, WS_TRADE_AMOUNT
    WS_TRADE_TYPE = 'SELL'
    WS_ORDER_TYPE = 'MARKET'
    WS_TRADE_AMOUNT = WS_SELL_AMOUNT
    trade_execution()

def create_buy_order():
    """11450-CREATE-BUY-ORDER."""
    global WS_TRADE_TYPE, WS_ORDER_TYPE, WS_BUY_AMOUNT, WS_TRADE_AMOUNT
    WS_TRADE_TYPE = 'BUY '
    WS_ORDER_TYPE = 'MARKET'
    WS_TRADE_AMOUNT = WS_BUY_AMOUNT
    trade_execution()

def generate_statements():
    """11500-GENERATE-STATEMENTS."""
    global WS_END_OF_QUARTER, WS_END_OF_YEAR
    monthly_statement()
    if WS_END_OF_QUARTER == 'Y':
        quarterly_report()
    if WS_END_OF_YEAR == 'Y':
        annual_tax_report()

    """11515-WRITE-HOLDINGS-DETAIL."""
    global WS_HOLD_IDX, WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT, HOLD_SYMBOL, HOLD_SHARES, HOLD_CURRENT_PRICE, HOLD_MARKET_VALUE, HOLD_

    def calc_auto_premium(self) -> None:
        """
        COBOL lines:
           EVALUATE TRUE
              WHEN 6 THRU 10
                 ADD 100 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN OTHER
                 ADD 50 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           END-EVALUATE
           IF WS-DRIVER-AGE < 25
              MULTIPLY 1.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           END-IF
           IF WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR > 0
              COMPUTE WS-ACCIDENT-SURCHARGE = 
                 WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR * 200
              ADD WS-ACCIDENT-SURCHARGE TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           END-IF
           IF WS-VIOLATIONS-3YR > 0
              COMPUTE WS-VIOLATION-SURCHARGE = 
                 WS-VIOLATIONS-3YR * 100
              ADD WS-VIOLATION-SURCHARGE TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           END-IF
           MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM
           COMPUTE WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM = 
              WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM / 12.
        """
        match True:
            case _ if 6 <= self.WS_DRIVER_AGE <= 10:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += Decimal("100.00")
            case _:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += Decimal("50.00")

        if self.WS_DRIVER_AGE < 25:
            self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5")

        if self.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR > 0:
            self.WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE = Decimal(self.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR * 200)
            self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += self.WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE

        if self.WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR > 0:
            self.WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE = Decimal(self.WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR * 100)
            self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += self.WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE

        self.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM
        self.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = self.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM / Decimal("12.0")

    def calc_home_premium(self) -> None:
        """
        13230-CALC-HOME-PREMIUM.
           COMPUTE WS-BASE-PREMIUM = 
              WS-COVERAGE-AMOUNT * 0.003
           EVALUATE WS-HOME-AGE
              WHEN 0 THRU 10
                 MULTIPLY 0.9 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN 11 THRU 25
                 MULTIPLY 1.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN 26 THRU 50
                 MULTIPLY 1.2 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN OTHER
                 MULTIPLY 1.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           END-EVALUATE
           IF WS-FLOOD-ZONE = 'Y'
              MULTIPLY 1.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           END-IF
           IF WS-SECURITY-SYSTEM = 'Y'
              MULTIPLY 0.9 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           END-IF
           COMPUTE WS-DEDUCTIBLE-CREDIT = 
              WS-DEDUCTIBLE / 1000 * 50
           SUBTRACT WS-DEDUCTIBLE-CREDIT FROM WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           IF WS-BASE-PREMIUM < 200
              MOVE 200 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           END-IF
           MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM
           COMPUTE WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM = 
              WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM / 12.
        """
        self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = self.WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.003")

        match self.WS_HOME_AGE:
            case _ if 0 <= self.WS_HOME_AGE <= 10:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9")
            case _ if 11 <= self.WS_HOME_AGE <= 25:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0")
            case _ if 26 <= self.WS_HOME_AGE <= 50:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.2")
            case _:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5")

        if self.WS_FLOOD_ZONE == 'Y':
            self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5")

        if self.WS_SECURITY_SYSTEM == 'Y':
            self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9")

        self.WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT = self.WS_DEDUCTIBLE / Decimal("1000.0") * Decimal("50.0")
        self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM -= self.WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT

        if self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM < Decimal("200.0"):
            self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal("200.00")

        self.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM
        self.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = self.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM / Decimal("12.0")

    def calc_health_premium(self) -> None:
        """
       13240-CALC-HEALTH-PREMIUM.
           MOVE 300 TO WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           EVALUATE WS-INSURED-AGE
              WHEN 0 THRU 18
                 MULTIPLY 0.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN 19 THRU 30
                 MULTIPLY 1.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN 31 THRU 40
                 MULTIPLY 1.3 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN 41 THRU 50
                 MULTIPLY 1.6 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN 51 THRU 60
                 MULTIPLY 2.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN OTHER
                 MULTIPLY 2.8 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           END-EVALUATE
           EVALUATE WS-PLAN-TYPE
              WHEN 'BRONZE'
                 MULTIPLY 0.8 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN 'SILVER'
                 MULTIPLY 1.0 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN 'GOLD'
                 MULTIPLY 1.3 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
              WHEN 'PLATINUM'
                 MULTIPLY 1.6 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           END-EVALUATE
           IF WS-FAMILY-PLAN = 'Y'
              MULTIPLY 2.5 BY WS-BASE-PREMIUM
           END-IF
           MOVE WS-BASE-PREMIUM TO WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM
           COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM = 
              WS-MONTHLY-PREMIUM * 12.
        """
        self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal("300.00")

        match self.WS_INSURED_AGE:
            case _ if 0 <= self.WS_INSURED_AGE <= 18:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.5")
            case _ if 19 <= self.WS_INSURED_AGE <= 30:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0")
            case _ if 31 <= self.WS_INSURED_AGE <= 40:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.3")
            case _ if 41 <= self.WS_INSURED_AGE <= 50:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.6")
            case _ if 51 <= self.WS_INSURED_AGE <= 60:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.0")
            case _:
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.8")

        match self.WS_PLAN_TYPE:
            case 'BRONZE':
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.8")
            case 'SILVER':
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0")
            case 'GOLD':
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.3")
            case 'PLATINUM':
                self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.6")

        if self.WS_FAMILY_PLAN == 'Y':
            self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.5")

        self.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = self.WS_BASE_PREMIUM
        self.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = self.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM * Decimal("12.0")

    def underwriting(self) -> None:
        """
       13300-UNDERWRITING.
           PERFORM 13310-EVALUATE-RISK-FACTORS
           PERFORM 13320-CHECK-MEDICAL-HISTORY
           PERFORM 13330-VERIFY-INFORMATION
           PERFORM 13340-DETERMINE-DECISION.
        """
        self.evaluate_risk_factors()  # 13310-EVALUATE-RISK-FACTORS
        self.check_medical_history()  # 13320-CHECK-MEDICAL-HISTORY
        self.verify_information()  # 13330-VERIFY-INFORMATION
        self.determine_decision()  # 13340-DETERMINE-DECISION

    def evaluate_risk_factors(self) -> None:
        """
       13310-EVALUATE-RISK-FACTORS.
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RISK-POINTS
           IF POLICY-LIFE
              IF WS-BMI > 30
                 ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
              END-IF
              IF WS-SMOKER-FLAG = 'Y'
                 ADD 25 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
              END-IF
              IF WS-HAZARDOUS-OCCUPATION = 'Y'
                 ADD 15 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
              END-IF
           END-IF
           IF POLICY-AUTO
              IF WS-DRIVER-AGE < 21
                 ADD 20 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
              END-IF
              IF WS-ACCIDENTS-3YR > 1
                 ADD 15 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
              END-IF
           END-IF.
        """
        self.WS_RISK_POINTS = 0

        if self.POLICY_LIFE:
            if self.WS_BMI > Decimal("30.0"):
                self.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10
            if self.WS_SMOKER_FLAG == 'Y':
                self.WS_RISK_POINTS += 25
            if self.WS_HAZARDOUS_OCCUPATION == 'Y':
                self.WS_RISK_POINTS += 15

        if self.POLICY_AUTO:
            if self.WS_DRIVER_AGE < 21:
                self.WS_RISK_POINTS += 20
            if self.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR > 1:
                self.WS_RISK_POINTS += 15

    def check_medical_history(self) -> None:
        """
       13320-CHECK-MEDICAL-HISTORY.
           IF WS-CHRONIC-CONDITIONS > 0
              COMPUTE WS-CONDITION-POINTS = 
                 WS-CHRONIC-CONDITIONS * 5
              ADD WS-CONDITION-POINTS TO WS-RISK-POINTS
           END-IF
           IF WS-RECENT-HOSPITALIZATION = 'Y'
              ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
           END-IF
           IF WS-PRESCRIPTION-COUNT > 5
              ADD 5 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
           END-IF.
        """
        if self.WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS > 0:
            self.WS_CONDITION_POINTS = Decimal(self.WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS * 5)
            self.WS_RISK_POINTS += int(self.WS_CONDITION_POINTS)  # COBOL does implicit conversion

        if self.WS_RECENT_HOSPITALIZATION == 'Y':
            self.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10

        if self.WS_PRESCRIPTION_COUNT > 5:
            self.WS_RISK_POINTS += 5

    def verify_information(self) -> None:
        """
       13330-VERIFY-INFORMATION.
           PERFORM 13335-CHECK-FRAUD-INDICATORS
           PERFORM 13336-VALIDATE-DOCUMENTS.
        """
        self.check_fraud_indicators()  # 13335-CHECK-FRAUD-INDICATORS
        self.validate_documents()  # 13336-VALIDATE-DOCUMENTS

    def check_fraud_indicators(self) -> None:
        """
       13335-CHECK-FRAUD-INDICATORS.
           IF WS-RECENT-CLAIMS > 3
              ADD 20 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-FRAUD-FLAG
           END-IF
           IF WS-ADDRESS-MISMATCH = 'Y'
              ADD 10 TO WS-RISK-POINTS
           END-IF.
        """
        if self.WS_RECENT_CLAIMS > 3:
            self.WS_RISK_POINTS += 20
            self.WS_FRAUD_FLAG = 'Y'

        if self.WS_ADDRESS_MISMATCH == 'Y':
            self.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10

    def validate_documents(self) -> None:
        """
       13336-VALIDATE-DOCUMENTS.
           IF WS-DOC-MISSING = 'Y'
              MOVE 'PENDING' TO WS-UW-STATUS
           ELSE
              MOVE 'COMPLETE' TO WS-UW-STATUS
           END-IF.
        """
        if self.WS_DOC_MISSING == 'Y':
            self.WS_UW_STATUS = 'PENDING'
        else:
            self.WS_UW_STATUS = 'COMPLETE'

    def determine_decision(self) -> None:
        """
       13340-DETERMINE-DECISION.
           EVALUATE TRUE
              WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 50
                 MOVE 'DECLINE' TO WS-UW-DECISION
              WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 30
                 MOVE 'SUBSTANDARD' TO WS-UW-DECISION
                 COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM = 
                    WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM * 1.5
              WHEN WS-RISK-POINTS > 15
                 MOVE 'STANDARD' TO WS-UW-DECISION
              WHEN OTHER
                 MOVE 'PREFERRED' TO WS-UW-DECISION
                 COMPUTE WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM = 
                    WS-ANNUAL-PREMIUM * 0.9
           END-EVALUATE.
        """
        match True:
            case _ if self.WS_RISK_POINTS > 50:
                self.WS_UW_DECISION = 'DECLINE'
            case _ if self.WS_RISK_POINTS > 30:
                self.WS_UW_DECISION = 'SUBSTANDARD'
                self.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5")
            case _ if self.WS_RISK_POINTS > 15:
                self.WS_UW_DECISION = 'STANDARD'
            case _:
                self.WS_UW_DECISION = 'PREFERRED'
                self.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9")

    def issue_policy(self) -> None:
        """
       13400-ISSUE-POLICY.
           IF WS-UW-DECISION NOT = 'DECLINE'
              PERFORM 13410-GENERATE-POLICY-NUMBER
              PERFORM 13420-CREATE-POLICY-RECORD
              PERFORM 13430-SET-BENEFICIARIES
              PERFORM 13440-SEND-POLICY-DOCS
           ELSE
              PERFORM 13450-SEND-DECLINE-LETTER
           END-IF.
        """
        if self.WS_UW_DECISION != 'DECLINE':
            self.generate_policy_number()  # 13410-GENERATE-POLICY-NUMBER
            self.create_policy_record()  # 13420-CREATE-POLICY-RECORD
            self.set_beneficiaries()  # 13430-SET-BENEFICIARIES
            self.send_policy_docs()  # 13440-SEND-POLICY-DOCS
        else:
            self.send_decline_letter()  # 13450-SEND-DECLINE-LETTER

    def generate_policy_number(self) -> None:
        """
       13410-GENERATE-POLICY-NUMBER.
           MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-DATE-PART
           MOVE WS-POLICY-TYPE TO WS-TYPE-PART
           COMPUTE WS-RANDOM-PART = 
              FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999
           STRING WS-TYPE-PART DELIMITED SIZE
                  WS-DATE-PART DELIMITED SIZE
                  WS-RANDOM-PART DELIMITED SIZE
              INTO WS-POLICY-NUMBER.
        """
        self.WS_DATE_PART = date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")  # Assuming Y

    def update_pep_status(self, pep_match_score: Decimal):
        """
        MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PEP-STATUS
        MOVE PEP-MATCH-SCORE TO WS-PEP-SCORE
        """
        self.ws_pep_status = 'Y'
        self.ws_pep_score = pep_match_score

    def check_adverse_media(self):
        """
        16116-CHECK-ADVERSE-MEDIA.
        MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO MEDIA-SEARCH-NAME
        CALL 'MEDIASRCH' USING MEDIA-REQUEST MEDIA-RESPONSE
        IF MEDIA-HITS-FOUND > 0
           ADD MEDIA-HITS-FOUND TO WS-WATCHLIST-HITS
        END-IF.
        """
        media_search_name = self.ws_customer_name  # Assuming media_search_name is a local variable
        MEDIASRCH(self.media_request, self.media_response)
        if self.media_response.media_hits_found > 0:
            self.ws_watchlist_hits += self.media_response.media_hits_found

    def calculate_match_score(self):
        """
        16120-CALCULATE-MATCH-SCORE.
        IF WS-OFAC-SCORE > 0
           ADD WS-OFAC-SCORE TO WS-MATCH-SCORE
        END-IF
        IF WS-PEP-SCORE > 0
           ADD WS-PEP-SCORE TO WS-MATCH-SCORE
        END-IF
        COMPUTE WS-MATCH-SCORE = 
           WS-MATCH-SCORE / WS-WATCHLIST-HITS.
        """
        if self.ws_ofac_score > 0:
            self.ws_match_score += self.ws_ofac_score
        if self.ws_pep_score > 0:
            self.ws_match_score += self.ws_pep_score
        if self.ws_watchlist_hits > 0:
            self.ws_match_score = self.ws_match_score / self.ws_watchlist_hits
        else:
            self.ws_match_score = Decimal('0.00')

    def determine_disposition(self):
        """
        16130-DETERMINE-DISPOSITION.
        EVALUATE TRUE
           WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 90
              MOVE 'CONFIRMED' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-SAR-REQUIRED
           WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 75
              MOVE 'POTENTIAL' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
              MOVE 'REVIEW' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
           WHEN WS-MATCH-SCORE >= 50
              MOVE 'WEAK' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
              MOVE 'CLEARED' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
           WHEN OTHER
              MOVE 'FALSE POSITIVE' TO WS-MATCH-TYPE
              MOVE 'CLEARED' TO WS-CASE-STATUS
        END-EVALUATE.
        """
        match self.ws_match_score:
            case score if score >= 90:
                self.ws_match_type = 'CONFIRMED'
                self.ws_sar_required = 'Y'
            case score if score >= 75:
                self.ws_match_type = 'POTENTIAL'
                self.ws_case_status = 'REVIEW'
            case score if score >= 50:
                self.ws_match_type = 'WEAK'
                self.ws_case_status = 'CLEARED'
            case _:
                self.ws_match_type = 'FALSE POSITIVE'
                self.ws_case_status = 'CLEARED'

    def kyc_verification(self):
        """
        16200-KYC-VERIFICATION.
        PERFORM 16210-VERIFY-IDENTITY
        PERFORM 16220-VERIFY-ADDRESS
        PERFORM 16230-VERIFY-DOCUMENTS
        PERFORM 16240-DETERMINE-KYC-STATUS.
        """
        self.verify_identity()
        self.verify_address()
        self.verify_documents()
        self.determine_kyc_status()

    def verify_identity(self):
        """
        16210-VERIFY-IDENTITY.
        MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-SSN TO ID-VERIFY-SSN
        MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-DOB TO ID-VERIFY-DOB
        MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO ID-VERIFY-NAME
        CALL 'IDVERIFY' USING ID-REQUEST ID-RESPONSE
        IF ID-VERIFIED = 'Y'
           MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-ID-STATUS
        ELSE
           MOVE 'FAILED' TO WS-ID-STATUS
        END-IF.
        """
        id_verify_ssn = self.ws_customer_ssn # Assuming id_verify_ssn is a local variable
        id_verify_dob = self.ws_customer_dob # Assuming id_verify_dob is a local variable
        id_verify_name = self.ws_customer_name # Assuming id_verify_name is a local variable
        IDVERIFY(self.id_request, self.id_response)
        if self.id_response.id_verified == 'Y':
            self.ws_id_status = 'VERIFIED'
        else:
            self.ws_id_status = 'FAILED'

    def verify_address(self):
        """
        16220-VERIFY-ADDRESS.
        MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-ADDRESS TO ADDR-VERIFY-INPUT
        CALL 'ADDRVERIFY' USING ADDR-REQUEST ADDR-RESPONSE
        IF ADDR-VERIFIED = 'Y'
           MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-ADDR-STATUS
        ELSE
           MOVE 'UNVERIFIED' TO WS-ADDR-STATUS
        END-IF.
        """
        addr_verify_input = self.ws_customer_address # Assuming addr_verify_input is a local variable
        ADDRVERIFY(self.addr_request, self.addr_response)
        if self.addr_response.addr_verified == 'Y':
            self.ws_addr_status = 'VERIFIED'
        else:
            self.ws_addr_status = 'UNVERIFIED'

    def verify_documents(self):
        """
        16230-VERIFY-DOCUMENTS.
        IF WS-DOC-TYPE = 'PASSPORT'
           PERFORM 16232-VERIFY-PASSPORT
        ELSE IF WS-DOC-TYPE = 'LICENSE'
           PERFORM 16234-VERIFY-LICENSE
        ELSE
           PERFORM 16236-VERIFY-OTHER-DOC
        END-IF
        END-IF.
        """
        if self.ws_doc_type == 'PASSPORT':
            self.verify_passport()
        elif self.ws_doc_type == 'LICENSE':
            self.verify_license()
        else:
            self.verify_other_doc()

    def verify_passport(self):
        """
        16232-VERIFY-PASSPORT.
        MOVE WS-PASSPORT-NUMBER TO PASSPORT-VERIFY-NUM
        MOVE WS-PASSPORT-COUNTRY TO PASSPORT-VERIFY-COUNTRY
        CALL 'PASSVERIFY' USING PASSPORT-REQ PASSPORT-RESP
        IF PASSPORT-VALID = 'Y'
           MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-DOC-STATUS
        ELSE
           MOVE 'INVALID' TO WS-DOC-STATUS
        END-IF.
        """
        passport_verify_num = self.ws_passport_number # Assuming passport_verify_num is a local variable
        passport_verify_country = self.ws_passport_country # Assuming passport_verify_country is a local variable
        PASSVERIFY(self.passport_req, self.passport_resp)
        if self.passport_resp.passport_valid == 'Y':
            self.ws_doc_status = 'VERIFIED'
        else:
            self.ws_doc_status = 'INVALID'

    def verify_license(self):
        """
        16234-VERIFY-LICENSE.
        MOVE WS-LICENSE-NUMBER TO LICENSE-VERIFY-NUM
        MOVE WS-LICENSE-STATE TO LICENSE-VERIFY-STATE
        CALL 'LICVERIFY' USING LICENSE-REQ LICENSE-RESP
        IF LICENSE-VALID = 'Y'
           MOVE 'VERIFIED' TO WS-DOC-STATUS
        ELSE
           MOVE 'INVALID' TO WS-DOC-STATUS
        END-IF.
        """
        license_verify_num = self.ws_license_number # Assuming license_verify_num is a local variable
        license_verify_state = self.ws_license_state # Assuming license_verify_state is a local variable
        LICVERIFY(self.license_req, self.license_resp)
        if self.license_resp.license_valid == 'Y':
            self.ws_doc_status = 'VERIFIED'
        else:
            self.ws_doc_status = 'INVALID'

    def verify_other_doc(self):
        """
        16236-VERIFY-OTHER-DOC.
        MOVE 'MANUAL REVIEW' TO WS-DOC-STATUS.
        """
        self.ws_doc_status = 'MANUAL REVIEW'

    def determine_kyc_status(self):
        """
        16240-DETERMINE-KYC-STATUS.
        IF WS-ID-STATUS = 'VERIFIED' AND
           WS-ADDR-STATUS = 'VERIFIED' AND
           WS-DOC-STATUS = 'VERIFIED'
           MOVE 'APPROVED' TO WS-KYC-STATUS
        ELSE
           MOVE 'PENDING' TO WS-KYC-STATUS
        END-IF.
        """
        if self.ws_id_status == 'VERIFIED' and \
           self.ws_addr_status == 'VERIFIED' and \
           self.ws_doc_status == 'VERIFIED':
            self.ws_kyc_status = 'APPROVED'
        else:
            self.ws_kyc_status = 'PENDING'

    def sanctions_check(self):
        """
        16300-SANCTIONS-CHECK.
        IF WS-SANCTIONS-HIT = 'Y'
           PERFORM 16310-ESCALATE-TO-COMPLIANCE
           PERFORM 16320-FREEZE-ACCOUNT
        END-IF.
        """
        if self.ws_sanctions_hit == 'Y':
            self.escalate_to_compliance()
            self.freeze_account()

    def escalate_to_compliance(self):
        """
        16310-ESCALATE-TO-COMPLIANCE.
        INITIALIZE WS-ESCALATION-RECORD
        MOVE 'SANCTIONS HIT' TO ESC-REASON
        MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-ID TO ESC-CUSTOMER
        MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO ESC-DATE
        MOVE 'URGENT' TO ESC-PRIORITY
        WRITE ESCALATION-RECORD FROM WS-ESCALATION-RECORD.
        """
        self.escalation_record = EscalationRecord() # Simulating INITIALIZE
        self.escalation_record.esc_reason = 'SANCTIONS HIT'
        self.escalation_record.esc_customer = self.ws_customer_id
        self.escalation_record.esc_date = CURRENT_DATE()
        self.escalation_record.esc_priority = 'URGENT'
        # Assuming WRITE ESCALATION-RECORD writes to a file/database
        # In Python, you would handle this differently, e.g., writing to a log file or database

    def freeze_account(self):
        """
        16320-FREEZE-ACCOUNT.
        MOVE 'F' TO WS-ACCOUNT-STATUS
        MOVE 'SANCTIONS FREEZE' TO WS-FREEZE-REASON
        REWRITE ACCOUNT-RECORD.
        """
        self.ws_account_status = 'F'
        self.ws_freeze_reason = 'SANCTIONS FREEZE'
        # Assuming REWRITE ACCOUNT-RECORD updates a database record
        # In Python, you would handle this differently, e.g., using a database ORM

    def transaction_monitoring(self):
        """
        16400-TRANSACTION-MONITORING.
        PERFORM 16410-CHECK-VELOCITY
        PERFORM 16420-CHECK-PATTERNS
        PERFORM 16430-CHECK-HIGH-RISK
        PERFORM 16440-CALCULATE-RISK-SCORE.
        """
        self.check_velocity()
        self.check_patterns()
        self.check_high_risk()
        self.calculate_risk_score()

    def check_velocity(self):
        """
        16410-CHECK-VELOCITY.
        IF WS-DAILY-TRANS-COUNT > WS-VELOCITY-THRESHOLD
           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-VELOCITY-FLAG
           ADD 20 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
        END-IF
        IF WS-DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT > WS-AMOUNT-THRESHOLD
           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-AMOUNT-FLAG
           ADD 20 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
        END-IF.
        """
        if self.ws_daily_trans_count > self.ws_velocity_threshold:
            self.ws_velocity_flag = 'Y'
            self.ws_fraud_score += 20
        if self.ws_daily_trans_amount > self.ws_amount_threshold:
            self.ws_amount_flag = 'Y'
            self.ws_fraud_score += 20

    def check_patterns(self):
        """
        16420-CHECK-PATTERNS.
        IF WS-ROUND-AMOUNT-COUNT > 5
           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PATTERN-FLAG
           ADD 15 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
        END-IF
        IF WS-STRUCTURING-DETECTED = 'Y'
           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PATTERN-FLAG
           ADD 30 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
        END-IF.
        """
        if self.ws_round_amount_count > 5:
            self.ws_pattern_flag = 'Y'
            self.ws_fraud_score += 15
        if self.ws_structuring_detected == 'Y':
            self.ws_pattern_flag = 'Y'
            self.ws_fraud_score += 30

    def check_high_risk(self):
        """
        16430-CHECK-HIGH-RISK.
        IF WS-HIGH-RISK-COUNTRY = 'Y'
           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-LOCATION-FLAG
           ADD 25 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
        END-IF
        IF WS-NEW-DEVICE = 'Y'
           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-DEVICE-FLAG
           ADD 10 TO WS-FRAUD-SCORE
        END-IF.
        """
        if self.ws_high_risk_country == 'Y':
            self.ws_location_flag = 'Y'
            self.ws_fraud_score += 25
        if self.ws_new_device == 'Y':
            self.ws_device_flag = 'Y'
            self.ws_fraud_score += 10

    def calculate_risk_score(self):
        """
        16440-CALCULATE-RISK-SCORE.
        EVALUATE TRUE
           WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 80
              MOVE 'BLOCK' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-MANUAL-REVIEW
           WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 60
              MOVE 'REVIEW' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-MANUAL-REVIEW
           WHEN WS-FRAUD-SCORE >= 40
              MOVE 'MONITOR' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
           WHEN OTHER
              MOVE 'APPROVE' TO WS-FRAUD-DECISION
        END-EVALUATE.
        """
        match self.ws_fraud_score:
            case score if score >= 80:
                self.ws_fraud_decision = 'BLOCK'
                self.ws_manual_review = 'Y'
            case score if score >= 60:
                self.ws_fraud_decision = 'REVIEW'
                self.ws_manual_review = 'Y'
            case score if score >= 40:
                self.ws_fraud_decision = 'MONITOR'
            case _:
                self.ws_fraud_decision = 'APPROVE'

    def suspicious_activity_report(self):
        """
        16500-SUSPICIOUS-ACTIVITY-REPORT.
        IF WS-SAR-REQUIRED = 'Y'
           PERFORM 16510-GATHER-SAR-DATA
           PERFORM 16520-GENERATE-SAR
           PERFORM 16530-FILE-SAR
        END-IF.
        """
        if self.ws_sar_required == 'Y':
            self.gather_sar_data()
            self.generate_sar()
            self.file_sar()

    def gather_sar_data(self):
        """
        16510-GATHER-SAR-DATA.
        MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-NAME TO SAR-SUBJECT-NAME
        MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-ADDRESS TO SAR-SUBJECT-ADDR
        MOVE WS-CUSTOMER-SSN TO SAR-SUBJECT-SSN
        MOVE WS-TRANSACTION-AMOUNT TO SAR-AMOUNT

        """
        self.sar_subject_name = self.ws_customer_name
        self.sar_subject_addr = self.ws_customer_address
        self.sar_subject_ssn = self.ws_customer_ssn
        self.sar_amount = self.ws_transaction_amount
        self.sar_activity_date = CURRENT_DATE()

        self.ws_sar_record.sar_rec_addr = self.sar_subject_addr
        self.ws_sar_record.sar_rec_amount = self.sar_amount
        self.ws_sar_record.sar_rec_date = self.sar_activity_date
        self.ws_sar_record.sar_rec_narrative = 'SUSPICIOUS PATTERN DETECTED'

        """

def compute_next_run_date(run_frequency: str):
    """
    COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE based on run frequency.

    COBOL lines:
    FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 1
    WHEN 'WEEKLY'
    COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE = FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 7
    WHEN 'MONTHLY'
    COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE = FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 30
    WHEN 'QUARTERLY'
    COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE = FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 90
    WHEN 'YEARLY'
    COMPUTE WS-NEXT-RUN-DATE = FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-LAST-RUN-DATE) + 365
    END-EVALUATE.
    """
    global WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE
    match run_frequency:
        case 'WEEKLY':
            WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = integer_of_date(WS_LAST_RUN_DATE) + 7
        case 'MONTHLY':
            WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = integer_of_DATE(WS_LAST_RUN_DATE) + 30
        case 'QUARTERLY':
            WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = integer_of_DATE(WS_LAST_RUN_DATE) + 90
        case 'YEARLY':
            WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = integer_of_DATE(WS_LAST_RUN_DATE) + 365
        case _:
            WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = integer_of_date(WS_LAST_RUN_DATE) + 1
