def collect_metrics():
    """COBOL Line: 21100-COLLECT-METRICS."""
    collect_transaction_metrics() # COBOL: PERFORM 21110-COLLECT-TRANSACTION-METRICS
    collect_customer_metrics() # COBOL: PERFORM 21120-COLLECT-CUSTOMER-METRICS
    collect_performance_metrics() # COBOL: PERFORM 21130-COLLECT-PERFORMANCE-METRICS

def collect_transaction_metrics():
    """COBOL Line: 21110-COLLECT-TRANSACTION-METRICS."""
    global WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT, WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT, WS_AVG_TRANS_AMOUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG, TRANS_AMOUNT

    WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal("0.00") # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT
    WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT = 0 # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT
    WS_AVG_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal("0.00") # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO WS-AVG-TRANS-AMOUNT

    # COBOL: PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
    i: int = 0
    while WS_EOF_FLAG == 'N':
        try:
            ws_trans_rec: TransactionRecord = TRANSACTION_FILE[i] # COBOL: READ TRANSACTION-FILE INTO WS-TRANS-REC
            TRANS_AMOUNT = ws_trans_rec.TRANS_AMOUNT
            WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT += 1 # COBOL: ADD 1 TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT
            WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT += TRANS_AMOUNT # COBOL: ADD TRANS-AMOUNT TO WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT
        except IndexError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y' # COBOL: MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
        i += 1

    if WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT > 0:
        WS_AVG_TRANS_AMOUNT = WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT / WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT # COBOL: COMPUTE WS-AVG-TRANS-AMOUNT = WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT / WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N' # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

def collect_customer_metrics():
    """COBOL Line: 21120-COLLECT-CUSTOMER-METRICS."""
    global WS_ACTIVE_CUSTOMERS, WS_NEW_CUSTOMERS, WS_CHURNED_CUSTOMERS, WS_EOF_FLAG, CUST_STATUS, CUST_OPEN_DATE, CUST_CLOSE_DATE

    WS_ACTIVE_CUSTOMERS = 0 # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS
    WS_NEW_CUSTOMERS = 0 # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO WS-NEW-CUSTOMERS
    WS_CHURNED_CUSTOMERS = 0 # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO WS-CHURNED-CUSTOMERS

    # COBOL: PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
    i: int = 0
    while WS_EOF_FLAG == 'N':
        try:
            ws_cust_rec: CustomerRecord = CUSTOMER_FILE[i] # COBOL: READ CUSTOMER-FILE INTO WS-CUST-REC
            CUST_STATUS = ws_cust_rec.CUST_STATUS
            CUST_OPEN_DATE = ws_cust_rec.CUST_OPEN_DATE
            CUST_CLOSE_DATE = ws_cust_rec.CUST_CLOSE_DATE

            if CUST_STATUS == 'A':
                WS_ACTIVE_CUSTOMERS += 1 # COBOL: ADD 1 TO WS-ACTIVE-CUSTOMERS

            if CUST_OPEN_DATE >= WS_PERIOD_START:
                WS_NEW_CUSTOMERS += 1 # COBOL: ADD 1 TO WS-NEW-CUSTOMERS

            if CUST_CLOSE_DATE >= WS_PERIOD_START:
                WS_CHURNED_CUSTOMERS += 1 # COBOL: ADD 1 TO WS-CHURNED-CUSTOMERS
        except IndexError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y' # COBOL: MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
        i += 1

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N' # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

def collect_performance_metrics():
    """COBOL Line: 21130-COLLECT-PERFORMANCE-METRICS."""
    global WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL, WS_RESPONSE_COUNT, WS_AVG_RESPONSE_TIME, WS_EOF_FLAG, PERF_RESPONSE_TIME

    WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL = Decimal("0.00") # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL
    WS_RESPONSE_COUNT = 0 # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RESPONSE-COUNT

    # COBOL: PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
    i: int = 0
    while WS_EOF_FLAG == 'N':
        try:
            ws_perf_rec: PerfLogRecord = PERF_LOG_FILE[i] # COBOL: READ PERF-LOG-FILE INTO WS-PERF-REC
            PERF_RESPONSE_TIME = ws_perf_rec.PERF_RESPONSE_TIME

            WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL += PERF_RESPONSE_TIME # COBOL: ADD PERF-RESPONSE-TIME TO WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL
            WS_RESPONSE_COUNT += 1 # COBOL: ADD 1 TO WS-RESPONSE-COUNT
        except IndexError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y' # COBOL: MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
        i += 1

    if WS_RESPONSE_COUNT > 0:
        WS_AVG_RESPONSE_TIME = WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL / WS_RESPONSE_COUNT # COBOL: COMPUTE WS-AVG-RESPONSE-TIME = WS-RESPONSE-TIME-TOTAL / WS-RESPONSE-COUNT

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N' # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

def aggregate_data():
    """COBOL Line: 21200-AGGREGATE-DATA."""
    daily_aggregation() # COBOL: PERFORM 21210-DAILY-AGGREGATION
    weekly_aggregation() # COBOL: PERFORM 21220-WEEKLY-AGGREGATION
    monthly_aggregation() # COBOL: PERFORM 21230-MONTHLY-AGGREGATION

def daily_aggregation():
    """COBOL Line: 21210-DAILY-AGGREGATION."""
    global WS_DAILY_SUMMARY, WS_PROCESS_DATE, WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT, WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT, DAILY_DATE, DAILY_TRANS_COUNT, DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT, DAILY_DEPOSITS, DAILY_WITHDRAWALS

    WS_DAILY_SUMMARY = DailySummaryRecord() # COBOL: INITIALIZE WS-DAILY-SUMMARY
    DAILY_DATE = WS_PROCESS_DATE # COBOL: MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO DAILY-DATE
    DAILY_TRANS_COUNT = WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT # COBOL: MOVE WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT TO DAILY-TRANS-COUNT
    DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT = WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT # COBOL: MOVE WS-TOTAL-TRANS-AMOUNT TO DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT
    DAILY_DEPOSITS = Decimal("0.00") #PLACEHOLDER# COBOL: MOVE WS-TOTAL-DEPOSITS TO DAILY-DEPOSITS
    DAILY_WITHDRAWALS = Decimal("0.00") #PLACEHOLDER# COBOL: MOVE WS-TOTAL-WITHDRAWALS TO DAILY-WITHDRAWALS

    # COBOL: WRITE DAILY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-DAILY-SUMMARY
    DAILY_SUMMARY_FILE.append(WS_DAILY_SUMMARY) # Replace with actual file writing

def weekly_aggregation():
    """COBOL Line: 21220-WEEKLY-AGGREGATION."""
    global WS_DAY_OF_WEEK, WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY, WS_WEEK_NUMBER, WEEKLY_WEEK

    if WS_DAY_OF_WEEK == 7:
        WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY = WeeklySummaryRecord() # COBOL: INITIALIZE WS-WEEKLY-SUMMARY
        WEEKLY_WEEK = WS_WEEK_NUMBER # COBOL: MOVE WS-WEEK-NUMBER TO WEEKLY-WEEK
        sum_week_data() # COBOL: PERFORM 21225-SUM-WEEK-DATA

        # COBOL: WRITE WEEKLY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-WEEKLY-SUMMARY
        WEEKLY_SUMMARY_FILE.append(WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY) # Replace with actual file writing

def sum_week_data():
    """COBOL Line: 21225-SUM-WEEK-DATA."""
    global WEEKLY_TRANS_COUNT, WEEKLY_TRANS_AMOUNT, DAILY_TRANS_COUNT, DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT

    WEEKLY_TRANS_COUNT = 0 # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO WEEKLY-TRANS-COUNT
    WEEKLY_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal("0.00") # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO WEEKLY-TRANS-AMOUNT

    # COBOL: PERFORM 7 TIMES
    for _ in range(7):
        WEEKLY_TRANS_COUNT += DAILY_TRANS_COUNT # COBOL: ADD DAILY-TRANS-COUNT TO WEEKLY-TRANS-COUNT
        WEEKLY_TRANS_AMOUNT += DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT # COBOL: ADD DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT TO WEEKLY-TRANS-AMOUNT

def monthly_aggregation():
    """COBOL Line: 21230-MONTHLY-AGGREGATION."""
    global WS_END_OF_MONTH, WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY, WS_CURR_MONTH, WS_CURR_YEAR, MONTHLY_MONTH, MONTHLY_YEAR

    if WS_END_OF_MONTH == 'Y':
        WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY = MonthlySummaryRecord() # COBOL: INITIALIZE WS-MONTHLY-SUMMARY
        MONTHLY_MONTH = WS_CURR_MONTH # COBOL: MOVE WS-CURR-MONTH TO MONTHLY-MONTH
        MONTHLY_YEAR = WS_CURR_YEAR # COBOL: MOVE WS-CURR-YEAR TO MONTHLY-YEAR
        sum_month_data() # COBOL: PERFORM 21235-SUM-MONTH-DATA

        # COBOL: WRITE MONTHLY-SUMMARY-RECORD FROM WS-MONTHLY-SUMMARY
        MONTHLY_SUMMARY_FILE.append(WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY) # Replace with actual file writing

def sum_month_data():
    """COBOL Line: 21235-SUM-MONTH-DATA."""
    global MONTHLY_TRANS_COUNT, MONTHLY_TRANS_AMOUNT, MONTHLY_NEW_ACCOUNTS, MONTHLY_CLOSED_ACCOUNTS, WS_EOF_FLAG, DAILY_MONTH, WS_CURR_MONTH, DAILY_TRANS_COUNT, DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT

    MONTHLY_TRANS_COUNT = 0 # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-TRANS-COUNT
    MONTHLY_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal("0.00") # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
    MONTHLY_NEW_ACCOUNTS = 0 # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-NEW-ACCOUNTS
    MONTHLY_CLOSED_ACCOUNTS = 0 # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO MONTHLY-CLOSED-ACCOUNTS

    # COBOL: PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
    i: int = 0
    while WS_EOF_FLAG == 'N':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec: DailySummaryRecord = DAILY_SUMMARY_FILE[i] # COBOL: READ DAILY-SUMMARY-FILE INTO WS-DAILY-SUM-REC
            DAILY_MONTH = ws_daily_sum_rec.DAILY_MONTH
            DAILY_TRANS_COUNT = ws_daily_sum_rec.DAILY_TRANS_COUNT
            DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT = ws_daily_sum_rec.DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT

            if DAILY_MONTH == WS_CURR_MONTH:
                MONTHLY_TRANS_COUNT += DAILY_TRANS_COUNT # COBOL: ADD DAILY-TRANS-COUNT TO MONTHLY-TRANS-COUNT
                MONTHLY_TRANS_AMOUNT += DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT # COBOL: ADD DAILY-TRANS-AMOUNT TO MONTHLY-TRANS-AMOUNT
        except IndexError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y' # COBOL: MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
        i += 1

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N' # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG

def calculate_kpi():
    """COBOL Line: 21300-CALCULATE-KPI."""
    calc_financial_kpi() # COBOL: PERFORM 21310-CALC-FINANCIAL-KPI
    calc_operational_kpi() # COBOL: PERFORM 21320-CALC-OPERATIONAL-KPI
    calc_customer_kpi() # COBOL: PERFORM 21330-CALC-CUSTOMER-KPI

def calc_financial_kpi():
    """COBOL Line: 21310-CALC-FINANCIAL-KPI."""
    global WS_TOTAL_ASSETS, WS_NET_INCOME, WS_ROA, WS_TOTAL_EQUITY, WS_ROE, WS_INTEREST_EXPENSE, WS_INTEREST_INCOME, WS_NIM, WS_EARNING_ASSETS

    if WS_TOTAL_ASSETS > 0:
        WS_ROA = (WS_NET_INCOME / WS_TOTAL_ASSETS) * 100 # COBOL: COMPUTE WS-ROA = (WS-NET-INCOME / WS-TOTAL-ASSETS) * 100

    if WS_TOTAL_EQUITY > 0:
        WS_ROE = (WS_NET_INCOME / WS_TOTAL_EQUITY) * 100 # COBOL: COMPUTE WS-ROE = (WS-NET-INCOME / WS-TOTAL-EQUITY) * 100

    if WS_INTEREST_EXPENSE > 0:
        WS_NIM = ((WS_INTEREST_INCOME - WS_INTEREST_EXPENSE) / WS_EARNING_ASSETS) * 100 # COBOL: COMPUTE WS-NIM = ((WS-INTEREST-INCOME - WS-INTEREST-EXPENSE) / WS-EARNING-ASSETS) * 100

def calc_operational_kpi():
    """COBOL Line: 21320-CALC-OPERATIONAL-KPI."""
    global WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT, WS_ERROR_COUNT, WS_ERROR_RATE, WS_SLA_COMPLIANCE, WS_WITHIN_SLA_COUNT, WS_TOTAL_CASES, WS_FIRST_CALL_RESOLUTION, WS_FCR_COUNT, WS_TOTAL_CALLS

    if WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT > 0:
        WS_ERROR_RATE = (WS_ERROR_COUNT / WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT) * 100 # COBOL: COMPUTE WS-ERROR-RATE = (WS-ERROR-COUNT / WS-TOTAL-TRANS-COUNT) * 100

    WS_SLA_COMPLIANCE = (WS_WITHIN_SLA_COUNT / WS_TOTAL_CASES) * 100 # COBOL: COMPUTE WS-SLA-COMPLIANCE = (WS-WITHIN-SLA-COUNT / WS-TOTAL-CASES) * 100
    WS_FIRST_CALL_RESOLUTION = (WS_FCR_COUNT / WS_TOTAL_CALLS) * 100

class ACH_INPUT_FILE:
    def __init__(self, records: list):
        self.records = records
        self.index = 0

    def read(self):
        if self.index < len(self.records):
            record = self.records[self.index]
            self.index += 1
            return record
        else:
            return None

    def close(self):
        pass

class ACH_RETURN_FILE:
    def __init__(self):
        self.records = []

    def write(self, record):
        self.records.append(record)

    def close(self):
        pass

    def main(self):
        """Main execution point"""
        if datetime.now().day <= 15:
            self.SHIP_METHOD = 'EXPRESS' # MOVE 'EXPRESS' TO SHIP-METHOD
            self.SHIP_EST_DELIVERY = INTEGER_OF_DATE(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 2 # COMPUTE SHIP-EST-DELIVERY = FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-PROCESS-DATE) + 2
        else:
            self.SHIP_METHOD = 'STANDARD' # MOVE 'STANDARD' TO SHIP-METHOD
            self.SHIP_EST_DELIVERY = INTEGER_OF_DATE(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 7 # COMPUTE SHIP-EST-DELIVERY = FUNCTION INTEGER-OF-DATE(WS-PROCESS-DATE) + 7
        # WRITE SHIPMENT-RECORD FROM WS-SHIPMENT-RECORD.
        print(f"Shipment Method: {self.SHIP_METHOD}, Estimated Delivery: {self.SHIP_EST_DELIVERY}")
        self.CARD_BLOCKING()
        self.WIRE_TRANSFER()
        self.ACH_PROCESSING()
        self.STATEMENT_GENERATION()
        self.OVERDRAFT_PROTECTION()
        self.INTEREST_ACCRUAL()
        self.STOP_PAYMENT()

    def CARD_BLOCKING(self):
        """23500-CARD-BLOCKING."""
        self.CARD_STATUS = 'B' # MOVE 'B' TO CARD-STATUS
        self.CARD_BLOCK_REASON = self.WS_BLOCK_REASON # MOVE WS-BLOCK-REASON TO CARD-BLOCK-REASON
        self.CARD_BLOCK_DATE = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE # MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO CARD-BLOCK-DATE
        # REWRITE CARD-RECORD FROM WS-CARD-RECORD
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'CARD-BLOCKED' # MOVE 'CARD-BLOCKED' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'SMS' # MOVE 'SMS' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
        self.WS_NOTIF_BODY = f'Your card has been blocked: {self.WS_BLOCK_REASON}' # STRING 'Your card has been blocked: ' DELIMITED SIZE WS-BLOCK-REASON DELIMITED SIZE INTO WS-NOTIF-BODY
        self.SEND_NOTIFICATION() # PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION

    def WIRE_TRANSFER(self):
        """24000-WIRE-TRANSFER."""
        self.VALIDATE_WIRE_REQUEST() # PERFORM 24100-VALIDATE-WIRE-REQUEST
        if self.WS_WIRE_VALID == 'Y': # IF WS-WIRE-VALID = 'Y'
            self.OFAC_SCREENING() # PERFORM 24200-OFAC-SCREENING
            if self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR == 'Y': # IF WS-OFAC-CLEAR = 'Y'
                self.PROCESS_WIRE() # PERFORM 24300-PROCESS-WIRE
                self.SEND_CONFIRMATION() # PERFORM 24400-SEND-CONFIRMATION
            else: # ELSE
                self.REJECT_WIRE() # PERFORM 24500-REJECT-WIRE

    def VALIDATE_WIRE_REQUEST(self):
        """24100-VALIDATE-WIRE-REQUEST."""
        self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
        if self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT <= 0: # IF WS-WIRE-AMOUNT <= 0
            self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
            self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INVALID AMOUNT' # MOVE 'INVALID AMOUNT' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
        if self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE: # IF WS-WIRE-AMOUNT > WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
            self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
            self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS' # MOVE 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
        if self.WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT == '': # IF WS-BENEFICIARY-ACCOUNT = SPACES
            self.WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-WIRE-VALID
            self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED' # MOVE 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
        if self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > 10000: # IF WS-WIRE-AMOUNT > 10000
            self.WS_CTR_REQUIRED = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CTR-REQUIRED

    def OFAC_SCREENING(self):
        """24200-OFAC-SCREENING."""
        self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'Y' # MOVE 'Y' TO WS-OFAC-CLEAR
        self.OFAC_REQUEST.ofac_search_name = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME # MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-NAME TO OFAC-SEARCH-NAME
        OFACSRCH(str(self.OFAC_REQUEST), str(self.OFAC_RESPONSE)) # CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING OFAC-REQUEST OFAC-RESPONSE
        if self.OFAC_RESPONSE.ofac_match_found == 'Y': # IF OFAC-MATCH-FOUND = 'Y'
            if self.OFAC_RESPONSE.ofac_match_score >= 85: # IF OFAC-MATCH-SCORE >= 85
                self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-OFAC-CLEAR
                self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'OFAC MATCH' # MOVE 'OFAC MATCH' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT
        self.OFAC_REQUEST.ofac_search_bank = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK # MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-BANK TO OFAC-SEARCH-BANK
        OFACSRCH(str(self.OFAC_REQUEST), str(self.OFAC_RESPONSE)) # CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING OFAC-REQUEST OFAC-RESPONSE
        if self.OFAC_RESPONSE.ofac_match_found == 'Y': # IF OFAC-MATCH-FOUND = 'Y'
            if self.OFAC_RESPONSE.ofac_match_score >= 85: # IF OFAC-MATCH-SCORE >= 85
                self.WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N' # MOVE 'N' TO WS-OFAC-CLEAR
                self.WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BANK OFAC MATCH' # MOVE 'BANK OFAC MATCH' TO WS-WIRE-REJECT

    def PROCESS_WIRE(self):
        """24300-PROCESS-WIRE."""
        self.DEBIT_ORIGINATOR() # PERFORM 24310-DEBIT-ORIGINATOR
        self.CREATE_WIRE_MESSAGE() # PERFORM 24320-CREATE-WIRE-MESSAGE
        self.TRANSMIT_WIRE() # PERFORM 24330-TRANSMIT-WIRE
        self.RECORD_WIRE() # PERFORM 24340-RECORD-WIRE

    def DEBIT_ORIGINATOR(self):
        """24310-DEBIT-ORIGINATOR."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT # SUBTRACT WS-WIRE-AMOUNT FROM WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_WIRE_FEE # SUBTRACT WS-WIRE-FEE FROM WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        self.UPDATE_ACCOUNT() # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT

    def CREATE_WIRE_MESSAGE(self):
        """24320-CREATE-WIRE-MESSAGE."""
        self.SWIFT_MESSAGE = SwiftMessage() # INITIALIZE WS-SWIFT-MESSAGE
        self.SWIFT_MESSAGE.swift_msg_type = 'MT103' # MOVE 'MT103' TO SWIFT-MSG-TYPE
        self.SWIFT_MESSAGE.swift_txn_ref = self.WS_WIRE_REF # MOVE WS-WIRE-REF TO SWIFT-TXN-REF
        self.SWIFT_MESSAGE.swift_value_date = self.WS_WIRE_DATE # MOVE WS-WIRE-DATE TO SWIFT-VALUE-DATE
        self.SWIFT_MESSAGE.swift_currency = self.WS_WIRE_CURRENCY # MOVE WS-WIRE-CURRENCY TO SWIFT-CURRENCY
        self.SWIFT_MESSAGE.swift_amount = self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT # MOVE WS-WIRE-AMOUNT TO SWIFT-AMOUNT
        self.SWIFT_MESSAGE.swift_ordering_cust = self.WS_ORIGINATOR_NAME # MOVE WS-ORIGINATOR-NAME TO SWIFT-ORDERING-CUST
        self.SWIFT_MESSAGE.swift_ordering_acct = self.WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT # MOVE WS-ORIGINATOR-ACCOUNT TO SWIFT-ORDERING-ACCT
        self.SWIFT_MESSAGE.swift_benef_cust = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME # MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-NAME TO SWIFT-BENEF-CUST
        self.SWIFT_MESSAGE.swift_benef_acct = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT # MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-ACCOUNT TO SWIFT-BENEF-ACCT
        self.SWIFT_MESSAGE.swift_benef_bank = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK # MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-BANK-BIC TO SWIFT-BENEF-BANK
        self.SWIFT_MESSAGE.swift_remit_info = self.WS_PURPOSE # MOVE WS-PURPOSE TO SWIFT-REMIT-INFO

    def TRANSMIT_WIRE(self):
        """24330-TRANSMIT-WIRE."""
        SWIFTSEND(self.WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE, self.WS_SWIFT_RESPONSE) # CALL 'SWIFTSEND' USING WS-SWIFT-MESSAGE WS-SWIFT-RESPONSE
        if self.SWIFT_STATUS == 'ACK': # IF SWIFT-STATUS = 'ACK'
            self.WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'SENT' # MOVE 'SENT' TO WS-WIRE-STATUS
        else: # ELSE
            self.WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'FAILED' # MOVE 'FAILED' TO WS-WIRE-STATUS
            self.REVERSE_DEBIT() # PERFORM 24350-REVERSE-DEBIT

    def RECORD_WIRE(self):
        """24340-RECORD-WIRE."""
        self.WS_WIRE_RECORD = WsWireRecord() # INITIALIZE WS-WIRE-RECORD
        self.WS_WIRE_RECORD.wire_ref = self.WS_WIRE_REF # MOVE WS-WIRE-REF TO WIRE-REF
        self.WS_WIRE_RECORD.wire_amount = self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT # MOVE WS-WIRE-AMOUNT TO WIRE-AMOUNT
        self.WS_WIRE_RECORD.wire_status = self.WS_WIRE_STATUS # MOVE WS-WIRE-STATUS TO WIRE-STATUS
        self.WS_WIRE_RECORD.wire_from_acct = self.WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT # MOVE WS-ORIGINATOR-ACCOUNT TO WIRE-FROM-ACCT
        self.WS_WIRE_RECORD.wire_to_acct = self.WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT # MOVE WS-BENEFICIARY-ACCOUNT TO WIRE-TO-ACCT
        self.WS_WIRE_RECORD.wire_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE # MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO WIRE-DATE
        # WRITE WIRE-RECORD FROM WS-WIRE-RECORD

    def REVERSE_DEBIT(self):
        """24350-REVERSE-DEBIT."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += self.WS_WIRE_AMOUNT # ADD WS-WIRE-AMOUNT TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += self.WS_WIRE_FEE # ADD WS-WIRE-FEE TO WS-ACCOUNT-BALANCE
        self.UPDATE_ACCOUNT() # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT

    def SEND_CONFIRMATION(self):
        """24400-SEND-CONFIRMATION."""
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'WIRE-CONFIRM' # MOVE 'WIRE-CONFIRM' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL' # MOVE 'EMAIL' TO WS-NOTIF-CHANNEL
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'Wire transfer {self.WS_WIRE_REF} completed' # STRING 'Wire transfer ' DELIMITED SIZE WS-WIRE-REF DELIMITED SIZE ' completed' DELIMITED SIZE INTO WS-NOTIF-SUBJECT
        self.SEND_NOTIFICATION() # PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION

    def REJECT_WIRE(self):
        """24500-REJECT-WIRE."""
        self.WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'REJECTED' # MOVE 'REJECTED' TO WS-WIRE-STATUS
        self.WS_WIRE_REJECT_REC = WsWireRejectRec() # INITIALIZE WS-WIRE-REJECT-REC
        self.WS_WIRE_REJECT_REC.reject_wire_ref = self.WS_WIRE_REF # MOVE WS-WIRE-REF TO REJECT-WIRE-REF
        self.WS_WIRE_REJECT_REC.reject_reason = self.WS_WIRE_REJECT # MOVE WS-WIRE-REJECT TO REJECT-REASON
        self.WS_WIRE_REJECT_REC.reject_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE # MOVE WS-PROCESS-DATE TO REJECT-DATE
        # WRITE WIRE-REJECT-RECORD FROM WS-WIRE-REJECT-REC
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'WIRE-REJECTED' # MOVE 'WIRE-REJECTED' TO WS-NOTIF-TYPE
        self.SEND_NOTIFICATION() # PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION

    def ACH_PROCESSING(self):
        """25000-ACH-PROCESSING."""
        self.RECEIVE_ACH_FILE() # PERFORM 25100-RECEIVE-ACH-FILE
        self.VALIDATE_ACH_ENTRIES() # PERFORM 25200-VALIDATE-ACH-ENTRIES
        self.PROCESS_ACH_CREDITS() # PERFORM 25300-PROCESS-ACH-CREDITS
        self.PROCESS_ACH_DEBITS() # PERFORM 25400-PROCESS-ACH-DEBITS
        self.GENERATE_ACH_RETURN() # PERFORM 25500-GENERATE-ACH-RETURN

    def RECEIVE_ACH_FILE(self):
        """25100-RECEIVE-ACH-FILE."""
        # OPEN INPUT ACH-INPUT-FILE
        ach_header = self.ACH_INPUT_FILE.read()
        if ach_header:
            self.WS_ACH_FILE_HEADER = ach_header
            self.ACH_FILE_ID = self.WS_ACH_FILE_HEADER.ach_file_id # MOVE ACH-FILE-ID TO WS-CURRENT-ACH-FILE
            self.ACH_CREATION_DATE = self.WS_ACH_FILE_HEADER.ach_creation_date # MOVE ACH-CREATION-DATE TO WS-ACH-FILE-DATE
            self.ACH_ENTRY_COUNT = self.WS_ACH_FILE_HEADER.ach_entry_count # MOVE ACH-ENTRY-COUNT TO WS-EXPECTED-ENTRIES
            self.WS_CURRENT_ACH_FILE = self.ACH_FILE_ID
            self.WS_ACH_FILE_DATE = self.ACH_CREATION_DATE
            self.WS_EXPECTED_ENTRIES = self.ACH_ENTRY_COUNT
        else:
            print("Error: Could not read ACH file header.")

    def VALIDATE_ACH_ENTRIES(self):
        """25200-VALIDATE-ACH-ENTRIES."""
        self.WS_VALID_ENTRIES = 0 # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-VALID-ENTRIES
        self.WS_INVALID_ENTRIES = 0 # MOVE ZEROES TO WS-INVALID-ENTRIES

        self.WS_FILE_RESULT: str = ""
        self.WS_FILE_NAME: str = ""
        self.WS_FILE_ERROR_LOG: WsFileErrorLog = WsFileErrorLog()
        self.WS_LUHN_SUM: int = 0
        self.WS_LUHN_IDX: int = 0
        self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT: int = 0
        self.WS_LUHN_VALID: str = ""
        self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED: str = ""
        self.WS_CVV_VALID: str = ""
        self.WS_CVV_RESULT: str = ""

    def validate_stop_request(self):
        """29100-VALIDATE-STOP-REQUEST."""
        self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'Y'
        if self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER == "0" * len(self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER):
            self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK NUMBER REQUIRED'
        if self.WS_CHECK_ALREADY_CLEARED == 'Y':
            self.WS_STOP_VALID = 'N'
            self.WS_STOP_REJECT = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED'

    def create_stop_order(self):
        """29200-CREATE-STOP-ORDER."""
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD = WsStopRecord()
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_account = self.ACCT_ID
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_check_number = self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_amount = self.WS_CHECK_AMOUNT
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_payee = self.WS_PAYEE_NAME
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_effective_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_expiry_date = self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 180
        self.WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_status = 'A'
        self.write_stop_record(self.WS_STOP_RECORD) # Assuming write_stop_record exists

    def apply_stop_fee(self):
        """29300-APPLY-STOP-FEE."""
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_STOP_PAYMENT_FEE
        self.update_account() # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'STOP-PAYMENT'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'Stop payment placed on check # {self.WS_CHECK_NUMBER}'
        self.send_notification() # PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION

    def safe_deposit_box(self):
        """30000-SAFE-DEPOSIT-BOX."""
        self.box_rental() # PERFORM 30100-BOX-RENTAL
        self.box_access() # PERFORM 30200-BOX-ACCESS
        self.box_drilling() # PERFORM 30300-BOX-DRILLING
        self.box_billing() # PERFORM 30400-BOX-BILLING

    def box_rental(self):
        """30100-BOX-RENTAL."""
        if self.WS_RENTAL_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.check_availability() # PERFORM 30110-CHECK-AVAILABILITY
            if self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE == 'Y':
                self.assign_box() # PERFORM 30120-ASSIGN-BOX
                self.create_rental_agreement() # PERFORM 30130-CREATE-RENTAL-AGREEMENT

    def check_availability(self):
        """30110-CHECK-AVAILABILITY."""
        self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'N'
        self.WS_BOX_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_BOX_IDX <= self.WS_TOTAL_BOXES:
            if self.BOX_STATUS.get(self.WS_BOX_IDX) == 'A':
                if self.BOX_SIZE.get(self.WS_BOX_IDX) == self.WS_REQUESTED_SIZE:
                    self.WS_BOX_AVAILABLE = 'Y'
                    self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX = str(self.WS_BOX_IDX)
                    break
            self.WS_BOX_IDX += 1

    def assign_box(self):
        """30120-ASSIGN-BOX."""
        self.BOX_STATUS[int(self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX)] = 'R'
        self.BOX_RENTER[int(self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX)] = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        self.BOX_RENTAL_DATE[int(self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX)] = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE

    def create_rental_agreement(self):
        """30130-CREATE-RENTAL-AGREEMENT."""
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT = WsRentalAgreement()
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_box_number = self.WS_ASSIGNED_BOX
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_customer = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_start_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_annual_fee = self.WS_BOX_SIZE_FEE.get(self.WS_REQUESTED_SIZE, Decimal("0.00"))
        self.write_rental_record(self.WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT) # Assuming write_rental_record exists

    def box_access(self):
        """30200-BOX-ACCESS."""
        if self.WS_ACCESS_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.verify_renter() # PERFORM 30210-VERIFY-RENTER
            if self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                self.log_access() # PERFORM 30220-LOG-ACCESS
                self.escort_to_vault() # PERFORM 30230-ESCORT-TO-VAULT

    def verify_renter(self):
        """30210-VERIFY-RENTER."""
        self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'N'
        if self.BOX_RENTER.get(int(self.WS_BOX_NUMBER)) == self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID:
            if self.WS_ID_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                if self.WS_KEY_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                    self.WS_RENTER_VERIFIED = 'Y'

    def log_access(self):
        """30220-LOG-ACCESS."""
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG = WsAccessLog()
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_box_number = self.WS_BOX_NUMBER
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_customer = self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_time = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%H%M%S")  #FUNCTION CURRENT-TIME
        self.WS_ACCESS_LOG.access_type = 'ENTRY'
        self.write_access_log_record(self.WS_ACCESS_LOG) # Assuming write_access_log_record exists

    def escort_to_vault(self):
        """30230-ESCORT-TO-VAULT."""
        self.WS_DISPLAY_MSG = 'VAULT ACCESS GRANTED'
        print(self.WS_DISPLAY_MSG) #DISPLAY WS-DISPLAY-MSG

    def box_drilling(self):
        """30300-BOX-DRILLING."""
        if self.WS_DRILLING_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.validate_drilling_auth() # PERFORM 30310-VALIDATE-DRILLING-AUTH
            if self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED == 'Y':
                self.schedule_drilling() # PERFORM 30320-SCHEDULE-DRILLING
                self.notify_renter() # PERFORM 30330-NOTIFY-RENTER

    def validate_drilling_auth(self):
        """30310-VALIDATE-DRILLING-AUTH."""
        self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'N'
        if self.WS_RENT_DELINQUENT_MONTHS >= 12:
            self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'
        if self.WS_COURT_ORDER == 'Y':
            self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'
        if self.WS_DECEASED_RENTER == 'Y':
            if self.WS_EXECUTOR_VERIFIED == 'Y':
                self.WS_DRILLING_AUTHORIZED = 'Y'

    def schedule_drilling(self):
        """30320-SCHEDULE-DRILLING."""
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD = WsDrillingRecord()
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD.drill_box_number = self.WS_BOX_NUMBER
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD.drill_reason = self.DRILL_REASON
        self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD.drill_scheduled_date = self.integer_of_date(self.WS_PROCESS_DATE) + 30
        self.write_drilling_record(self.WS_DRILLING_RECORD) # Assuming write_drilling_record exists

    def notify_renter(self):
        """30330-NOTIFY-RENTER."""
        self.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'BOX-DRILLING'
        self.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'MAIL'
        self.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Important notice regarding your safe deposit box'
        self.send_notification() # PERFORM 15000-SEND-NOTIFICATION

    def box_billing(self):
        """30400-BOX-BILLING."""
        self.WS_BOX_IDX = 1
        while self.WS_BOX_IDX <= self.WS_TOTAL_BOXES:
            if self.BOX_STATUS.get(self.WS_BOX_IDX) == 'R':
                if self.BOX_RENEWAL_DUE.get(self.WS_BOX_IDX) == 'Y':
                    self.charge_annual_fee() # PERFORM 30410-CHARGE-ANNUAL-FEE
            self.WS_BOX_IDX += 1

    def charge_annual_fee(self):
        """30410-CHARGE-ANNUAL-FEE."""
        self.WS_CUSTOMER_ID = self.BOX_RENTER.get(self.WS_BOX_IDX)
        self.WS_FEE_AMOUNT = self.BOX_ANNUAL_FEE.get(self.WS_BOX_IDX, Decimal("0.00"))
        self.WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= self.WS_FEE_AMOUNT
        self.update_account() # PERFORM 2350-UPDATE-ACCOUNT
        self.BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[self.WS_BOX_IDX] = self.BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL.get(self.WS_BOX_IDX, 0) + 10000

    def merchant_services(self):
        """31000-MERCHANT-SERVICES."""
        self.process_authorization() # PERFORM 31100-PROCESS-AUTHORIZATION
        self.capture_transaction() # PERFORM 31200-CAPTURE-TRANSACTION
        self.process_settlement() # PERFORM 31300-PROCESS-SETTLEMENT
        self.handle_chargeback() # PERFORM 31400-HANDLE-CHARGEBACK

    def process_authorization(self):
        """31100-PROCESS-AUTHORIZATION."""
        self.validate_card() # PERFORM 31110-VALIDATE-CARD
        if self.WS_CARD_VALID == 'Y':
            self.check_fraud_score() # PERFORM 31120-CHECK-FRAUD-SCORE
            if self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED == 'Y':
                self.check_available_credit() # PERFORM 31130-CHECK-AVAILABLE-CREDIT
                if self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE == 'Y':
                    self.approve_auth() # PERFORM 31140-APPROVE-AUTH
                else:
                    self.decline_auth() # PERFORM 31150-DECLINE-AUTH
            else:
                self.decline_auth() # PERFORM 31150-DECLINE-AUTH
        else:
            self.decline_auth() # PERFORM 31150-DECLINE-AUTH

    def validate_card(self):
        """31110-VALIDATE-CARD."""
        self.WS_CARD_VALID = 'N'
        self.check_luhn() # PERFORM 31115-CHECK-LUHN
        if self.WS_LUHN_VALID == 'Y':
            self.check_expiry() # PERFORM 31116-CHECK-EXPIRY
            if self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED == 'Y':
                self.check_cvv() # PERFORM 31117-CHECK-CVV
                if self.WS_CVV_VALID == 'Y':
                    self.WS_CARD_VALID = 'Y'

    def check_luhn(self):
        """31115-CHECK-LUHN."""
        self.WS_LUHN_SUM = 0
        self.WS_LUHN_IDX = 16
        while self.WS_LUHN_IDX >= 1:
            self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT = int(self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER[self.WS_LUHN_IDX - 1]) # Access character by index
            if (17 - self.WS_LUHN_IDX) % 2 == 0:
                self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT *= 2
                if self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT > 9:
                    self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT -= 9
            self.WS_LUHN_SUM += self.WS_LUHN_DIGIT
            self.WS_LUHN_IDX -= 1

        if self.WS_LUHN_SUM % 10 == 0:
            self.WS_LUHN_VALID = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_LUHN_VALID = 'N'

    def check_expiry(self):
        """31116-CHECK-EXPIRY."""
        if self.WS_AUTH_EXPIRY_DATE >= self.WS_PROCESS_DATE:
            self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_NOT_EXPIRED = 'N'

    def check_cvv(self):
        """31117-CHECK-CVV."""
        self.cvvverify(self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER, self.WS_AUTH_CVV) # CALL 'CVVVERIFY'
        if self.WS_CVV_RESULT == 'M':
            self.WS_CVV_VALID = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_CVV_VALID = 'N'

    def check_fraud_score(self):
        """31120-CHECK-FRAUD-SCORE."""
        self.fraudcheck(self.WS_AUTH_REQUEST) # CALL 'FRAUDCHECK'
        if self.FRAUD_SCORE < 70:
            self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_FRAUD_APPROVED = 'N'
            self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = self.FRAUD_DECLINE_CODE

    def check_available_credit(self):
        """31130-CHECK-AVAILABLE-CREDIT."""
        self.WS_SEARCH_KEY = self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
        self.read_card_account_file() # READ CARD-ACCOUNT-FILE
        if self.WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT >= self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT:
            self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'Y'
        else:
            self.WS_CREDIT_AVAILABLE = 'N'
            self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE = '51'

    def approve_auth(self):
        """31140-APPROVE-AUTH."""
        self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE = '00'
        self.generate_auth_code() # PERFORM 31145-GENERATE-AUTH-CODE
        self.WS_AVAILABLE_CREDIT -= self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
        self.record_authorization() # PERFORM 31146-RECORD-AUTHORIZATION

    def generate_auth_code(self):
        """31145-GENERATE-AUTH-CODE."""
        self.WS_AUTH_CODE = int(random.random() * 999999)
        self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE = str(self.WS_AUTH_CODE)

    def record_authorization(self):
        """31146-RECORD-AUTHORIZATION."""
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD = WsAuthRecord()
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_card = self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_amount = self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_code = self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_AUTH_CODE
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_time = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%H%M%S") #FUNCTION CURRENT-TIME
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_merchant = self.WS_MERCHANT_ID
        self.WS_AUTH_RECORD.auth_rec_status = 'P'
        self.write_auth_record(self.WS_AUTH_RECORD) # Assuming write_auth_record exists

    def decline_auth(self):
        """31150-DECLINE-AUTH."""
        self.WS_AUTH_RESPONSE_CODE = self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD = WsDeclineRecord()
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_rec_card = self.WS_AUTH_CARD_NUMBER
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_rec_amount = self.WS_AUTH_AMOUNT
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_rec_code = self.WS_AUTH_DECLINE_CODE
        self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD.decline_rec_date = self.WS_PROCESS_DATE
        self.write_decline_record(self.WS_DECLINE_RECORD) # Assuming write_decline_record exists

    def capture_transaction(self):
        """31200-CAPTURE-TRANSACTION."""
        if self.WS_CAPTURE_REQUEST == 'Y':
            self.validate_auth_code() # PERFORM 31210-VALIDATE-AUTH-CODE
            if self.WS_AUTH_VALID == 'Y':
                self.create_capture_record() # PERFORM 31220-CREATE-CAPTURE-RECORD

    def validate_auth_code(self):
        """31210-VALIDATE-AUTH-CODE."""
        self.WS_AUTH_VALID = 'N'
        self.AUTH_SEARCH

    def log_info(self):
        """99810-LOG-INFO."""
        self.log_level = 'INFO'                                   # MOVE 'INFO' TO LOG-LEVEL
        self.log_message = self.ws_log_message                       # MOVE WS-LOG-MESSAGE TO LOG-MESSAGE
        self.log_timestamp = str(datetime.now())                    # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO LOG-TIMESTAMP
        self.write_log_record()                              # WRITE LOG-RECORD FROM WS-LOG-ENTRY.

        """99820-LOG-WARNING."""
        self.log_level = 'WARN'                                    # MOVE 'WARN' TO LOG-LEVEL
        self.log_message = self.ws_log_message                       # MOVE WS-LOG-MESSAGE TO LOG-MESSAGE
        self.log_timestamp = str(datetime.now())                    # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO LOG-TIMESTAMP
        self.write_log_record()                              # WRITE LOG-RECORD FROM WS-LOG-ENTRY.

        """99830-LOG-ERROR."""

        self.log_message = self.ws_log_message                       # MOVE WS-LOG-MESSAGE TO LOG-MESSAGE
        self.log_timestamp = str(datetime.now())                    # MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO LOG-TIMESTAMP
        self.write_log_record()                              # WRITE LOG-RECORD FROM WS-LOG-ENTRY.

        """Dummy method for COBOL WRITE statement"""
        pass

    def update_cfp_status(self):
        """MOVE 'ADEQUATE' TO WS-CFP-STATUS
           END-IF."""
        self.WS_CFP_STATUS = 'ADEQUATE'

    def update_cfp_document(self):
        """33330-UPDATE-CFP-DOCUMENT.
           MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-CFP-UPDATE-DATE
           MOVE WS-CFP-STATUS TO CFP-OVERALL-STATUS
           MOVE WS-AVAILABLE-FUNDING TO CFP-TOTAL-SOURCES
           MOVE WS-STRESSED-OUTFLOWS TO CFP-STRESS-NEEDS
           REWRITE CFP-RECORD FROM WS-CFP-DOCUMENT."""
        self.WS_CFP_UPDATE_DATE = date.today()
        self.ws_cfp_document.cfp_overall_status = self.WS_CFP_STATUS
        self.ws_cfp_document.cfp_total_sources = self.WS_AVAILABLE_FUNDING
        self.ws_cfp_document.cfp_stress_needs = self.WS_STRESSED_OUTFLOWS
        # Assuming a method to write CFP-RECORD from WS-CFP-DOCUMENT
        self.rewrite_cfp_record(self.ws_cfp_document)


    def capital_management(self):
        """34000-CAPITAL-MANAGEMENT.
           PERFORM 34100-CALCULATE-CAPITAL-RATIOS
           PERFORM 34200-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
           PERFORM 34300-CAPITAL-PLANNING
           PERFORM 34400-STRESS-TESTING."""
        self.calculate_capital_ratios()
        self.risk_weighted_assets()
        self.capital_planning()
        self.stress_testing()

    def calculate_capital_ratios(self):
        """34100-CALCULATE-CAPITAL-RATIOS.
           PERFORM 34110-CALCULATE-TIER1
           PERFORM 34120-CALCULATE-TIER2
           PERFORM 34130-CALCULATE-RATIOS."""
        self.calculate_tier1()
        self.calculate_tier2()
        self.calculate_ratios()

    def calculate_tier1(self):
        """34110-CALCULATE-TIER1.
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
           ADD WS-COMMON-STOCK TO WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
           ADD WS-RETAINED-EARNINGS TO WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
           ADD WS-AOCI TO WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
           SUBTRACT WS-GOODWILL FROM WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
           SUBTRACT WS-INTANGIBLES FROM WS-TIER1-CAPITAL
           SUBTRACT WS-DTA-DEDUCTION FROM WS-TIER1-CAPITAL."""
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += self.WS_COMMON_STOCK
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += self.WS_RETAINED_EARNINGS
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += self.WS_AOCI
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= self.WS_GOODWILL
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= self.WS_INTANGIBLES
        self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= self.WS_DTA_DEDUCTION

    def calculate_tier2(self):
        """34120-CALCULATE-TIER2.
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-TIER2-CAPITAL
           ADD WS-SUB-DEBT TO WS-TIER2-CAPITAL
           ADD WS-ALLL-ELIGIBLE TO WS-TIER2-CAPITAL
           COMPUTE WS-TOTAL-CAPITAL = 
              WS-TIER1-CAPITAL + WS-TIER2-CAPITAL."""
        self.WS_TIER2_CAPITAL = Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_TIER2_CAPITAL += self.WS_SUB_DEBT
        self.WS_TIER2_CAPITAL += self.WS_ALLL_ELIGIBLE
        self.WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL = self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL + self.WS_TIER2_CAPITAL

    def calculate_ratios(self):
        """34130-CALCULATE-RATIOS.
           IF WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS > 0
              COMPUTE WS-CET1-RATIO = 
                 (WS-TIER1-CAPITAL / WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS) * 100
              COMPUTE WS-CAPITAL-RATIO = 
                 (WS-TOTAL-CAPITAL / WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS) * 100
           END-IF
           IF WS-TOTAL-ASSETS > 0
              COMPUTE WS-LEVERAGE-RATIO = 
                 (WS-TIER1-CAPITAL / WS-TOTAL-ASSETS) * 100
           END-IF."""
        if self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS > 0:
            self.WS_CET1_RATIO = (self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL / self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS) * 100
            self.WS_CAPITAL_RATIO = (self.WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL / self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS) * 100
        if self.WS_TOTAL_ASSETS > 0:
            self.WS_LEVERAGE_RATIO = (self.WS_TIER1_CAPITAL / self.WS_TOTAL_ASSETS) * 100

    def risk_weighted_assets(self):
        """34200-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS.
           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
           PERFORM 34210-CREDIT-RWA
           PERFORM 34220-MARKET-RWA
           PERFORM 34230-OPERATIONAL-RWA."""
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS = Decimal("0.00")
        self.credit_rwa()
        self.market_rwa()
        self.operational_rwa()

    def credit_rwa(self):
        """34210-CREDIT-RWA.
           COMPUTE WS-CASH-RWA = WS-CASH-POSITION * 0.00
           COMPUTE WS-GOVT-RWA = WS-GOVT-SECURITIES * 0.00
           COMPUTE WS-BANK-RWA = WS-BANK-DEPOSITS * 0.20
           COMPUTE WS-MORTGAGE-RWA = WS-RESIDENTIAL-MORTGAGES * 0.50
           COMPUTE WS-COMMERCIAL-RWA = WS-COMMERCIAL-LOANS * 1.00
           COMPUTE WS-CONSUMER-RWA = WS-CONSUMER-LOANS * 1.00
           ADD WS-CASH-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
           ADD WS-GOVT-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
           ADD WS-BANK-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
           ADD WS-MORTGAGE-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
           ADD WS-COMMERCIAL-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS
           ADD WS-CONSUMER-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS."""
        self.WS_CASH_RWA = self.WS_CASH_POSITION * Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_GOVT_RWA = self.WS_GOVT_SECURITIES * Decimal("0.00")
        self.WS_BANK_RWA = self.WS_BANK_DEPOSITS * Decimal("0.20")
        self.WS_MORTGAGE_RWA = self.WS_RESIDENTIAL_MORTGAGES * Decimal("0.50")
        self.WS_COMMERCIAL_RWA = self.WS_COMMERCIAL_LOANS * Decimal("1.00")
        self.WS_CONSUMER_RWA = self.WS_CONSUMER_LOANS * Decimal("1.00")
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_CASH_RWA
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_GOVT_RWA
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_BANK_RWA
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_MORTGAGE_RWA
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_COMMERCIAL_RWA
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_CONSUMER_RWA

    def market_rwa(self):
        """34220-MARKET-RWA.
           COMPUTE WS-MARKET-RWA = 
              WS-TRADING-ASSETS * WS-MARKET-RISK-FACTOR
           ADD WS-MARKET-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS."""
        self.WS_MARKET_RWA = self.WS_TRADING_ASSETS * self.WS_MARKET_RISK_FACTOR
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_MARKET_RWA

    def operational_rwa(self):
        """34230-OPERATIONAL-RWA.
           COMPUTE WS-OPERATIONAL-RWA = 
              WS-GROSS-INCOME * WS-OPERATIONAL-FACTOR * 12.5
           ADD WS-OPERATIONAL-RWA TO WS-RISK-WEIGHTED-ASSETS."""
        self.WS_OPERATIONAL_RWA = self.WS_GROSS_INCOME * self.WS_OPERATIONAL_FACTOR * Decimal("12.5")
        self.WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS += self.WS_OPERATIONAL_RWA

    def capital_planning(self):
        """34300-CAPITAL-PLANNING.
           PERFORM 34310-PROJECT-CAPITAL-NEEDS
           PERFORM 34320-IDENTIFY-CAPITAL-ACTIONS
           PERFORM 34330-UPDATE-CAPITAL-PLAN."""
        self.project_capital_needs()
        self.identify_capital_actions()
        self.update_capital_plan()

    def project_capital_needs(self):
        """34

WS_RECON_EXCEPTION: ReconException = ReconException()
WS_IC_DIFF_REC: IC_Diff_Rec = IC_Diff_Rec()

WS_IC_ARRAY: list[str] = [""] * 100 # Assuming max 100 entries
WS_NOSTRO_ITEM: str = "" # Place Holder since we do not know the structure

def main():
    """Main entry point.
    """
    global WS_GL_CONTROL_BAL, WS_SUBLEDGER_TOTAL, WS_RECON_DIFF
    # COBOL: WS-GL-CONTROL-BAL - WS-SUBLEDGER-TOTAL
    WS_RECON_DIFF = WS_GL_CONTROL_BAL - WS_SUBLEDGER_TOTAL
    # COBOL: IF WS-RECON-DIFF NOT = ZEROES
    if WS_RECON_DIFF != Decimal('0.00'):
        # COBOL: PERFORM 37235-LOG-RECON-EXCEPTION
        log_recon_exception()

# COBOL: 37235-LOG-RECON-EXCEPTION.
def log_recon_exception():
    """37235-LOG-RECON-EXCEPTION.
    """
    global WS_RECON_EXCEPTION, WS_GL_ACCOUNT, WS_RECON_DIFF
    # COBOL: INITIALIZE WS-RECON-EXCEPTION
    WS_RECON_EXCEPTION = ReconException()
    # COBOL: MOVE WS-GL-ACCOUNT TO RECON-EXC-ACCOUNT
    WS_RECON_EXCEPTION.RECON_EXC_ACCOUNT = WS_GL_ACCOUNT
    # COBOL: MOVE WS-RECON-DIFF TO RECON-EXC-DIFF
    WS_RECON_EXCEPTION.RECON_EXC_DIFF = WS_RECON_DIFF
    # COBOL: MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO RECON-EXC-DATE
    WS_RECON_EXCEPTION.RECON_EXC_DATE = str(datetime.now())
    # COBOL: WRITE RECON-EXCEPTION-RECORD FROM WS-RECON-EXCEPTION.
    write_recon_exception_record(WS_RECON_EXCEPTION)

def write_recon_exception_record(record: ReconException):
    """Placeholder for writing the recon exception record.
    """
    print(f"Writing RECON-EXCEPTION-RECORD: {record}")

# COBOL: 37300-INTERCOMPANY-RECON.
def intercompany_recon():
    """37300-INTERCOMPANY-RECON.
    """
    # COBOL: PERFORM 37310-LOAD-IC-BALANCES
    load_ic_balances()
    # COBOL: PERFORM 37320-MATCH-IC-PAIRS
    match_ic_pairs()
    # COBOL: PERFORM 37330-REPORT-IC-DIFFERENCES
    report_ic_differences()

# COBOL: 37310-LOAD-IC-BALANCES.
def load_ic_balances():
    """37310-LOAD-IC-BALANCES.
    """
    global WS_IC_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_IC_ARRAY, WS_IC_BALANCE

    # COBOL: MOVE ZEROES TO WS-IC-COUNT
    WS_IC_COUNT = 0
    # COBOL: PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            # COBOL: READ INTERCOMPANY-FILE INTO WS-IC-BALANCE
            WS_IC_BALANCE = read_intercompany_file()
            # COBOL: ADD 1 TO WS-IC-COUNT
            WS_IC_COUNT += 1
            # COBOL: MOVE WS-IC-BALANCE TO WS-IC-ARRAY(WS-IC-COUNT)
            WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_COUNT-1] = WS_IC_BALANCE
        except EOFError:
            # COBOL: AT END MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

    # COBOL: END-PERFORM
    # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def read_intercompany_file() -> str:
    """Placeholder for reading the INTERCOMPANY-FILE.
    Raises EOFError when end of file is reached.
    """
    # Simulate reading from a file
    global WS_IC_COUNT
    if WS_IC_COUNT < 5:  # Simulate 5 records
        return f"IC Record {WS_IC_COUNT + 1}"
    else:
        raise EOFError

# COBOL: 37320-MATCH-IC-PAIRS.
def match_ic_pairs():
    """37320-MATCH-IC-PAIRS.
    """
    global WS_IC_IDX, WS_IC_COUNT

    # COBOL: PERFORM VARYING WS-IC-IDX FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-IC-IDX > WS-IC-COUNT
    WS_IC_IDX = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX <= WS_IC_COUNT:
        # COBOL: PERFORM 37325-FIND-IC-COUNTERPART
        find_ic_counterpart()
        WS_IC_IDX += 1
    # COBOL: END-PERFORM.

# COBOL: 37325-FIND-IC-COUNTERPART.
def find_ic_counterpart():
    """37325-FIND-IC-COUNTERPART.
    """
    global WS_IC_IDX, WS_IC_IDX2, WS_IC_COUNT, WS_SEARCH_FROM, WS_SEARCH_TO, WS_IC_DIFF, WS_IC_ARRAY
    # COBOL: MOVE IC-FROM-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX) TO WS-SEARCH-FROM
    WS_SEARCH_FROM = get_ic_from_entity(WS_IC_IDX)
    # COBOL: MOVE IC-TO-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX) TO WS-SEARCH-TO
    WS_SEARCH_TO = get_ic_to_entity(WS_IC_IDX)
    # COBOL: PERFORM VARYING WS-IC-IDX2 FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-IC-IDX2 > WS-IC-COUNT
    WS_IC_IDX2 = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX2 <= WS_IC_COUNT:
        # COBOL: IF IC-FROM-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX2) = WS-SEARCH-TO
        if get_ic_from_entity(WS_IC_IDX2) == WS_SEARCH_TO:
            # COBOL: IF IC-TO-ENTITY(WS-IC-IDX2) = WS-SEARCH-FROM
            if get_ic_to_entity(WS_IC_IDX2) == WS_SEARCH_FROM:
                # COBOL: COMPUTE WS-IC-DIFF = IC-AMOUNT(WS-IC-IDX) + IC-AMOUNT(WS-IC-IDX2)
                WS_IC_DIFF = get_ic_amount(WS_IC_IDX) + get_ic_amount(WS_IC_IDX2)
                # COBOL: IF WS-IC-DIFF NOT = ZEROES
                if WS_IC_DIFF != Decimal('0.00'):
                    # COBOL: PERFORM 37326-LOG-IC-DIFF
                    log_ic_diff()
                # COBOL: END-IF
                # COBOL: EXIT PERFORM
                break
            # COBOL: END-IF
        # COBOL: END-IF
        WS_IC_IDX2 += 1
    # COBOL: END-PERFORM.

def get_ic_from_entity(idx: int) -> str:
    """Placeholder for getting IC-FROM-ENTITY.
    """
    return f"From Entity {idx}"

def get_ic_to_entity(idx: int) -> str:
    """Placeholder for getting IC-TO-ENTITY.
    """
    return f"To Entity {idx}"

def get_ic_amount(idx: int) -> Decimal:
    """Placeholder for getting IC-AMOUNT.
    """
    return Decimal(str(idx * 100))

# COBOL: 37326-LOG-IC-DIFF.
def log_ic_diff():
    """37326-LOG-IC-DIFF.
    """
    global WS_IC_DIFF_REC, WS_SEARCH_FROM, WS_SEARCH_TO, WS_IC_DIFF
    # COBOL: INITIALIZE WS-IC-DIFF-REC
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC = IC_Diff_Rec()
    # COBOL: MOVE WS-SEARCH-FROM TO ICD-FROM
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC.ICD_FROM = WS_SEARCH_FROM
    # COBOL: MOVE WS-SEARCH-TO TO ICD-TO
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC.ICD_TO = WS_SEARCH_TO
    # COBOL: MOVE WS-IC-DIFF TO ICD-AMOUNT
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC.ICD_AMOUNT = WS_IC_DIFF
    # COBOL: WRITE IC-DIFF-RECORD FROM WS-IC-DIFF-REC.
    write_ic_diff_record(WS_IC_DIFF_REC)

def write_ic_diff_record(record: IC_Diff_Rec):
    """Placeholder for writing the IC-DIFF-RECORD.
    """
    print(f"Writing IC-DIFF-RECORD: {record}")

# COBOL: 38000-AUDIT-TRAIL.
def audit_trail():
    """38000-AUDIT-TRAIL.
    """
    # COBOL: PERFORM 38100-LOG-USER-ACTION

    log_data_change()

# COBOL: 38100-LOG-USER-ACTION.
def log_user_action():
    """38100-LOG-USER-ACTION.
    """
    import random
    global WS_AUDIT_RECORD, WS_AUDIT_ID, WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP, WS_USER_ID, WS_ACTION_TYPE, WS_SESSION_ID, WS_AUDIT_REC
    # COBOL: INITIALIZE WS-AUDIT-RECORD
    WS_AUDIT_REC = AuditRecord()
    # COBOL: COMPUTE WS-AUDIT-ID = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999999999
    WS_AUDIT_REC.WS_AUDIT_ID = random.random() * 99999999999
    # COBOL: MOVE FUNCTION CURRENT-DATE TO WS-AUDIT-TIMESTAMP
    WS_AUDIT_REC.WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP = str(datetime.now())
    # COBOL: MOVE WS-USER-ID TO WS-AUDIT-USER
    WS_AUDIT_REC.WS_AUDIT_USER = WS_USER_ID
    # COBOL: MOVE WS-ACTION-TYPE TO WS-AUDIT-ACTION
    WS_AUDIT_REC.WS_AUDIT_ACTION = WS_ACTION_TYPE
    # COBOL: MOVE WS-SESSION-ID TO WS-AUDIT-SESSION-ID
    WS_AUDIT_REC.WS_AUDIT_SESSION_ID = WS_SESSION_ID
    # COBOL: WRITE AUDIT-RECORD FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD.
    write_audit_record(WS_AUDIT_REC)

def write_audit_record(record: AuditRecord):
    """Placeholder for writing the AUDIT-RECORD.

# COBOL: 38400-ARCHIVE-AUDIT-LOGS.
def archive_audit_logs():
    """38400-ARCHIVE-AUDIT-LOGS.
    """
    global WS_END_OF_MONTH

    # COBOL: IF WS-END-OF-MONTH = 'Y'
    if WS_END_OF_MONTH == 'Y':
        # COBOL: PERFORM 38410-MOVE-TO-ARCHIVE
        move_to_archive()
        # COBOL: PERFORM 38420-COMPRESS-ARCHIVE
        compress_archive()
    # COBOL: END-IF.

# COBOL: 38410-MOVE-TO-ARCHIVE.
def move_to_archive():
    """38410-MOVE-TO-ARCHIVE.
    """
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_AUDIT_RECORD, WS_ARCHIVE_DATE, WS_AUDIT_REC

    # COBOL: PERFORM UNTIL WS-EOF-FLAG = 'Y'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            # COBOL: READ AUDIT-FILE INTO WS-AUDIT-RECORD
            WS_AUDIT_REC = read_audit_file()
            # COBOL: IF WS-AUDIT-TIMESTAMP < WS-ARCHIVE-DATE
            if WS_AUDIT_REC.WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP < WS_ARCHIVE_DATE:
                # COBOL: WRITE ARCHIVE-AUDIT-RECORD FROM WS-AUDIT-RECORD
                write_archive_audit_record(WS_AUDIT_REC)
                # COBOL: DELETE AUDIT-FILE
                delete_audit_file()
            # COBOL: END-IF
        except EOFError:
            # COBOL: AT END MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    # COBOL: END-PERFORM
    # COBOL: MOVE 'N' TO WS-EOF-FLAG.
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def read_audit_file() -> AuditRecord:
    """Placeholder for reading the AUDIT-FILE.
    Raises EOFError when end of file is reached.
    """
    # Simulate reading from a file
    global WS_IC_COUNT
    if WS_IC_COUNT < 5:  # Simulate 5 records
        WS_IC_COUNT += 1
        return AuditRecord()
    else:
        raise EOFError

def write_archive_audit_record(record: AuditRecord):
    """Placeholder for writing the ARCHIVE-AUDIT-RECORD.
    """
    print(f"Writing ARCHIVE-AUDIT-RECORD: {record}")

def delete_audit_file():
    """Placeholder for deleting the AUDIT-FILE.
    """
    print("Deleting AUDIT-FILE")

# COBOL: 38420-COMPRESS-ARCHIVE.
def compress_archive():
    """38420-COMPRESS-ARCHIVE.
    """
    # COBOL: DISPLAY 'COMPRESSING AUDIT ARCHIVE'.
    print('COMPRESSING AUDIT ARCHIVE')

# COBOL: 39100-COLLECT-METRICS.
def collect_metrics():
    """39100-COLLECT-MET
